akkappōr 
akkappōr eṉpatu innāḷ potumakkaḷ vaḻakkuc col. kalviyaṟivu aṟavē illāta potu makkaḷiṭattut tōṉṟik kaṟṟōr mutal aṉivariṭattum ūṉṟiyuḷḷa col.
uṉṉōṭu eppōtum akkappōrāka irukkiṟatu. uṉṉōṭu akkappōr ceyya nammāl ākātu. Xahkš xÊahkš m¡f¥ngh® g©QgtndhL v‹d brŒtJ?; niṉṟālum kuṟṟam, naṭantālum kuṟṟam avaṉōṭu orē akkappōr- ippaṭi nāḷum poḻutum eṅkum evariṭamum kēṭkum col akkappōr! iv vakkappōrkkup poruḷ kāṇa vaḻakkiyali nōkkutal vēṇṭum.
akkam pakkam eṉpatu iṇimoḻi. ivaṟṟuḷ akkam, taṉvīṭum tāṉirukkum iṭamum cārntatu. pakkam taṉ vīṭṭukku aṭuttuḷḷa vīṭum, tāṉirukkum iṭattiṟkum aṭuttuḷḷa iṭamum.
akkam pakkam pārttup pēcu
eṉpatu īriṭaṅkaḷiyum iṇikkum paḻamoḻi.
pakalil pakkam pārttup pēcu; iravil atuvum pēcātē
eṉpatu maṟṟoru paḻamoḻi.
pakalilum, akkam paṟṟip pēcutal kūṭātu. pārttup pēcutal, pārātu pēcutal iraṇṭum kūṭātu. cuvarum kēṭkum, tōṭṭamum kēṭkum, kēṇiyum kēṭkum, muṟṟamum kēṭkum, mūli muṭukkum kēṭkum, etu eṅkiruntu eppaṭik kēṭkiṟatu eṉṟu teriyāmal kēṭkum appaṭik kēṭṭāl eṉṉa viḷivām? akkap pōrām!
kēṭṭati vittuk koṇṭu, vīṭṭukkuḷḷē vivatu pōla veḷiyāri viyalām; caṭṭi, pāṉi, āṭumāṭu, vēliyāḷ, vēṟṟāṉ evaric cākkiṭṭēṉum cāṭi māṭiyākap pēcalām! kēṭkap pēcalām. kēṭṭum kēḷāmalum pēcalām! iru vīṭṭārkkum - oṭṭukkuṭi ōrakkuṭi aṇṭi ayal, eṉṟu vāḻum iru cārārkkum - ōyāttolli! oḻiyāt tolli; ariciṟaṅku ōyntālum ōyāta aṭaṅkāta tolli! kiṇṭik kiḷaṟutalit toḻilākak koṇṭa kōḻi ataṉi viṭuttālum, taṉ iṉattic cērntatu aṭutta teru vaḻiyil vāli maṭakkik koṇṭu ōṭutalik kaṇṭu kurittali nāy maṟantālum, ayarti maṟatiyāl viṭṭālum - viṭāta muṇakal! mūcceṟivu! uruṭṭal puraṭṭal! itik kaṇṭa oruvar maṉatti icceyal vāṭṭiyirukkiṟatu. entap pōr iruntālum intap pōrkku - akkap pōrkku - iṭantarutal kūṭātu eṉa eṇṇi yiruppār. ataṉi oru collākki ulava viṭṭiruppār!
kiṭṭa uṟavu muṭṭap paki	eṉpati evar aṟiyār.
aṭuttiruntu māṇāta ceyvāṉ	-(tiruk. 867) 
pakiyic cuṭṭukiṟārē vaḷḷuvar. maṭiyilē pūṉiyik kaṭṭik koṇṭu cokiṉam (cakuṉam) pārppatā? eṉkiṟatē oru paḻamoḻi! pakkattuk kaṭaṉō paḻikkaṭaṉō eṉkiṟatē maṟṟoru paḻamoḻi!
iviyellām akkap pōrkku iṭantarātē akkap pōri vilikku vāṅkik koḷḷātē eṉṉum eṇṇattiṉ veḷippāṭukaḷē!
akkam, k ākiya oṟṟukkeṭa akam ākum. pakukkap paṭṭatu pakam, pākam. pākap pirivu; ataṟkup pirivu ellik kōṭu illāmal iruntāl oruvariṭamākavē iruntirukkum.
ivviḷakkaṅkaḷ eṉṉa colkiṉṟaṉa? kuṭi vaḻiyil vēṟoruvar iruntālum, akkappōr kuṟivāka irukkum. orē kuṭumpattavar - kurutik kalappuṭiyavar - akkappōrā nūṟāṇṭu vāḻvu; noṭi noṭiyum cāvu eṉa akkappōr ākkum eṉpati eccarikkiṟatām.
akkam 
akkam:1
akkam = tavacam.
aḵkutal > akkutal > ciṟitātal.
perumpālāṉa tavacaṅkaḷ ciṟiyavi. tiṉi, cāmi, varaku, kutirivāli, kāṭikkaṇṇi, kampu, kēḻvaraku eṉṉum tamiḻakattut tavacaṅkaḷ mikac ciṟiyaviyām.
cōḷam, nel eṉpavi caṟṟē peritāyiṉum paḻavaki, kāyvaki, payaṟuvaki (mocci, avari) kiḻaṅkuvaki pōlap periyavi alla.
akkam:2
puṟattē pulappaṭāmal akattē amintuḷḷa nīr akkam eṉappaṭum. teṉṉi, paṉi ākiyavaṟṟiṉ uḷḷē iruntu pāḷiyic cīviviṭac coṭṭuccoṭṭāka uḷḷiruntu vaḻiyum nīr akkāṉīr eṉṟu kumari māvaṭṭa vaḻakkil uḷḷatu. akkam eṉpatit taṇṇīr eṉpatu tañci vaḻakku. ūṟṟunīr, nilattakattu nīr allavā!
* aḵkam kāṇka.


akkam pakkam 
akkam    taṉvīṭum tāṉirukkum iṭamum.
pakkam taṉvīṭṭukku aṭuttuḷḷa vīṭum, tāṉirukkum iṭattiṟku aṭuttuḷḷa iṭamum
orukuṭivaḻiyar allatu tāyātiyar irukkum vīṭu vaḷivu, vaḷacal eṉappeṟum. avvīṭukaḷ oru kālattil ōruṭimiyāy iruntu piṉṉarp pala pākamāy amital vaḻakku. avaṟṟuḷ taṉ vīṭum iṭamum akkamum, ataṟku arukiluḷḷatu pakkamum āyiṉa. akkam = akam; pakkam = pakam (pirivu).
akkattiṟku - akattiṟkup - pakkam amintatu pakkam. aṭuttum toṭuttum amintavi avi.
akkari 
akkari eṉpatu a+kari eṉap pirikkappaṭṭu, antak kari eṉpatik kuṟikkum. kaṭal kaṭanta iṭam, akkaric cīmieṉappaṭum. ōvarcīcu pēṅku eṉpatu, akkari vaṅki allatu akkari vippakamākum.
ikkari māṭṭukku akkari paccieṉpatu paḻamoḻi. āṟṟiṉ akkariyil amintatōr ūr, akkarippaṭṭi! kācillātavaṉ ik kariyil kiṭantāl eṉṉa, akkariyil kiṭantāl eṉṉa?eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
akkaḷippu 
akam + kaḷippu = akakkaḷippu > akkaḷippu ; maṉamakiḻvu.
cakkaḷattikaḷ akkaḷippoṭu kekkalippaṭa 	- car. cama. kīr, 166
uḷḷak kaḷittal  eṉpatum itu.
uḷḷak kaḷittalum kāṇa makiḻtaluma	- tiruk. 1249
akkaṟi 
oṉṟaṉ mēl aḷavilā īṭupāṭu kāṭṭuvatum, atē nōkkamāka iruntu muyalvatum akkaṟi ākum.
akkaṟi eṉpati akkari eṉa eḻutuvārum uḷar. atu pōl appaṭi eḻuti iruvaṭivum kāṭṭum akarātiyum uṇṭu. ilakkiyattil kūṭa uṇṭu āṉāl ivaṟṟip paṭiyeṭuttōriṉ piḻiyeṉat taḷḷi viṭalām. iru vaḻakkākavum eḻutappaṭutal karuti, iru vaḻakkiyum akarātiyil ēṟṟiṉār eṉṟum taḷḷiviṭalām.
akkaṟi eṉpatu iraṭṭic collāl āya oṟṟic col. aṟivaṟi (aṟivu aṟi) eṉṟum kuṟiyaṟi (kuṟi aṟi) eṉṟum vaḻaṅkappaṭutal pōla, akku aṟi
 eṉap pirikkappaṭum col akkaṟi.
akku eṉpatu aḵku eṉṉum paḻa vaṭiviṉatu. aḵkutal kuṟital, karuṅkutal eṉap poruḷ taruvatu. aḵki akaṉṟa aṟivu(nuṇuki virinta aṟivu) eṉat tirukkuṟaḷil āṭci peṟum (175). aḵkāmi eṉa ataṉ etiriṭiyāyc curuṅkāmiyik kāṭṭiyum tirukkuṟaḷil āṭci peṟum (178)
aḵku eṉpatiluḷḷa āyta eḻuttu, nuṇukkam eṉṉum poruḷ taruvatu eṉpatu moḻiyiyal aṟintārum. tolkāppiyam kaṟṟārum teḷivāka aṟinta ceyti. accol akku eṉṟākiyum apporuḷ tarum.
aṟi eṉpataṉ poruḷ aṟuttal, aṟukkappaṭutal eṉṉum poruḷatu. vīṭṭiṉ aṟi, kaṇṇaṟi (kuḷattumaṭi), aṟikkīri, mūkkaṟi, kātaṟi eṉpavaṟṟil uḷḷa aṟiyiṉ poruḷ kāṇka. aṟi pōku kuṭikaḷ (ūri viṭṭu aṟṟup pōkum kuṭikaḷ) eṉpatu cilampu (4 10)
aṟivu aṟi pōtal, aṟivaṟṟup pōtal, kuṟi aṟi kuṟiyaṟṟup pōtal eṉṉum poruḷaṉa eṉpati eṇṇuka. āyiṉ, akkaṟi eṉpataṉ poruḷ eṉṉa?
akku eṉpataṟkuk kaṇṭa poruḷiyum, aṟi eṉpataṟkuk kaṇṭa poruḷiyum cērttāl, akkaṟip poruḷ vantuviṭum.
curukkam aṟṟup pōtal akkaṟi eṉka. oṉṟaṉ mēl perukkamāṉa īṭupāṭu, perukkamāṉa ārvam, perukkamāṉa uḻippu, perukkamāṉa untutal iruttaltāṉē, akkaṟi! apporuḷtāṉē ivviraṭṭic col iṇivām. oṟṟic coṟ poruḷ.
akkākkāy 
kuḻantikaḷukkuk kuḻal vāruvār kūṟum tiruppāṭal.
mātavaṉ taṉkuḻal vārāyak kākkāy	- nālā. 162
kākkā! kaṇṇukku mi koṇṭu vā
kuruvi! koṇṭikkup pūk koṇṭu vā
eṉpatu pōlap pāṭuvatu, kākkiyic cuṭṭik kāṭṭi vā eṉak kūṟalāl akkākkāy eṉappaṭṭatām.
akkāraṭali 
akkaram + aṭali = akkāraṭali
karuppukkaṭṭi vellakkaṭṭi cērttu aṭappaṭṭa - camikkap paṭṭa - cōṟu
ām pālakkā raṭali yaṇpaṉīrū ṟamirtam
* akkāṉīr kāṇka.
akkāraṭicil 
akkāram + aṭicil = akkāraṭicil
akkāram = carukkari; aṭicil = cōṟu.
ariciyum pācip paruppum camamākak kalantu nāṉku paṅku pālil camittuc carukkariyum neyyum mikak kalantu ceyvatu akkāra aṭicilām (ti.pi.a)
akki 
aḵki (alki) > akki
akki = viyarvikkuru (vērkkuru) pōl appit tōṉṟic civantu arippum tuyarum ūṭṭum tōl nōy. itaṟkuk kāvikkaṭṭiyi arittuk taṭavutal akki eḻututal eṉappaṭum. ataṟkeṉap paṇṭuvam pārppavar ciṟṟūrkaḷil uṇṭu.
akkiyam 
alkiyam = curukkattaṉmi īyāta karumiyi akkiyam piṭittavaṉ, aṭuttavaṉ koṭuppatum ivaṉukkuk kaṇṇeriyum. eṉpar. paḻikkappaṭum īyā ivaṟaṉ (karumi) ivaṉ.
karumittaṉam vēṟu, cikkaṉam vēṟu. tam tēviyic cīrāka eṇṇic cikkeṉak kaṭippiṭittu etirkālak kāppuk karutic celaviṭuvatu cikkaṉam. puṇṇukkut taṭavavum cuṇṇāmpu tarātavaṉkarumi.
akkuṇi 
alku + uṇi = alkuṇi > akkuṇi
ciṟitaḷavē uṇṇupavaṉ akkuṇi.
akkuṇip piḷḷikkut tukkiṇip picci
eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
akkut tokku 
akku 	= 	tavacam
tokku 	= 	paṇam
aḵkam (akkam) curukkēl eṉpatil akkam tavacamātal aṟika. akkamum kācum cikkeṉat tēṭu(auvi ātti). eṉpatilum akkam tavacameṉat teḷivākum.
tokku = tokukkap peṟṟatu; toki, payirkaḷil tavacappayir, paṇappayir eṉa iruvaki iruttal aṟika. akkut tokku illi eṉpatu marapu moḻi. tavacamum paṇamum illi eṉṟum, tavacamum paṇamum tantutavuvār ilar eṉṟum iraṇṭiyum kuṟikkum. iṉi uṟṟār uṟavu illi eṉpatiyum kuṟittal uṇṭu.
akku = uṟṟār; tokku = uṟavu
akku = nerukkam (akkam pakkam)
tokku = toṭakku > tokku = toṭuttu iruppatu.
akkuvēr āṇivēr 
akkuvēr =  melliya vēr
āṇivēr =  āḻntu cellum valiya vēr.
akkutal = curuṅkutal, melitātal, oru marattiṉ vērkaḷuḷ pakkattuc cellum vēr palavāy meliyavāy irukkum. āṇivēr nērkīḻ iṟaṅkuvatāy oṉṟāy valiyatāy irukkum.
akku vērāka āṇi vērāka āytal eṉpatu vaḻakku.
akkuvēṟu āṇivēṟu 
akku = muḷ
āṇi = kālaṭiyil tōṉṟiya kaṭṭi
muḷ tittu aḵtu eṭukkap paṭāmalē niṉṟu pōṉāl avviṭam kaṭṭi paṭṭuk kalpōl ākik kāli ūṉṟa muṭiyā valikku āḷākkum. piṉṉar avviṭatti akaḻntu kaṭṭiyiyum muḷḷiyum akaṟṟutal uṇṭu. ataṉi āṇi piṭuṅkutal eṉpar.
akkuḷuttal 
alku + uḷ = aḵkuḷ > akkuḷ.
tōṭpaṭṭiyiṉ uḷḷiṭamākiya akkuḷ, uṇarvu mika uṭiyatu. aṅkē kiviral koṇṭu varuṭiṉ nakippu ēṟpaṭum. kiccuk kāṭṭal eṉpatu akkuḷuttalām.
akkuḷuttup pullalum āṟṟēṉ	- kalit. 94
akkuḷukkāṭṭal eṉpatum itu. kiccuk kāṭṭal eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku. māṭṭu mutukil kiccuk kāṭṭal uḻavar vaḻakaku. vaṇṭiyi virintu ōṭṭa itaṉic ceyvar.
akkuṟumpu 
akam + kuṟumpu = akakkuṟumpu > akkuṟumpu.
kuṟumpu = ciṟutaṉamāṉa ceyal; vaṟaṇṭa kāṭṭu vaḻiyil celvāri vaḻimaṟittup paṟittuk kavarutalum, uṭaṉāki iruntē ciṟu ceyal ceytalum akkuṟumpu ākum (ma.va.)
akki 
akam > akkam; nerukkam
akku + ai = akki; uṭaṉpiṟanta mūttāḷ; mūttāḷ, ai = tāy.
tammi neruṅki irukkum - tammakattil uṭaṉirukkum - tāy = akki.
tam eṉpatu nerukkam kāṭṭuvatu. tam + akki = tamakki.
ō.nō 
tam + aiyaṉ = tamiyaṉ; aṇṇaṉ, mūttōṉ. ai = tanti
tāyōṭu iruppiṉum tāyiṉṟi iruppiṉum tāy niliyil iruppavar tamakkiyārām. tanti yōṭiruppiṉum tanti yiṉṟi iruppiṉum tanti niliyil iruppavar tamiyaṉārām.
aṅkaṇam 
aṅkaṇam: 1
aṅkaṇam pēccu, eḻuttu eṉṉum iruvaki vaḻakkukaḷilum iṉṟum vaḻaṅkum col. aṅkaṇak kuḻi, aṅkaṇat toṭṭi, aṅkaṇak kiṭaṅku eṉa ulaka vaḻakkilum
aṅkaṇattuḷ ukka amiḻtaṟṟāl	- tiruk. 720
ūraṅkaṇa nīr	- nālaṭi. 175
eṉa ilakkiya vaḻakkilum iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. camiyalaṟiyil kalaṅkaḷ kaḻuvum nīr, ki kaḻuvutal, tuṭittal ākiya nīr, vaḻivataṟkāka amikkappaṭṭa kuḻi allatu toṭṭi aṅkaṇakkuḻi, aṅkaṇattoṭṭi eṉappaṭum.
camiyaṟ pakutiyil aṭuppu uyaramum, atil tāḻntu taḷamum, attaḷattil tāḻntu aṅkaṇak kuḻiyum, ak kuḻiyiliruntu vaḻiyum nīr vaḻintōṭum paṭi vaṭikālum amikkappaṭutal eṇṇat takkaṉa.
aṅkaṇak kuḻiyil koṭṭappaṭum nīr veḷiyēṟu vataṟkut tuḷiyuṇṭu. tūmpum uṇṭu. tuḷi, puri, curi,kuḻi, puḻi, muḻi, nuḻi, vaḷi eṉpavi ellām tuḷi eṉṉum poruḷ taruvaṉavē.
tuḷikkuk kaṇ eṉpatu oru peyar. māṉ kaṇ pōlat tuḷi yamittuk kāṟṟup pukuvāy amittaṉar muntiyōr. atu māṉkaṇ kālatar māḷiki(cilap.5 8) eṉappaṭṭatu. kāl atar = kāṟṟu vaḻi.
kaṇ eṉpatāl aminta peyaruṭiyatu kaṇ vāy. kaṇṇē nīr vaḻiyum vāyāka amikkappaṭuvatalāl kaṇvāy eṉappaṭṭatu. atuvē innāḷ kammāy eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. māṉkaṇ pulikkaṇ nāḻikkaṇ tuṭuppuk kaṇ eṉa akkaṇkaḷ amikkap peṟṟuḷḷami kāṇak kūṭiyavē.
aṅkaṇak kuḻiyil iruntu nīr celvataṟkuk kaṇ allatu tuḷi amikkappaṭuvatu teḷivāṉa ceyti. akkaṇ veḷiyē pulappaṭa amintatā kuḻiyiṉuḷ maṟivāy amintatā? eṉiṉ, ataṉ akattē maṟivāka amikkap paṭṭatēyām. akam + kaṇ = aṅkaṇ; uḷḷiṭattē aminta kaṇ akaṅkaṇ  (aṅkaṇ). am eṉṉum oṭṭiṉic cērttu akaṅkaṇ am (aṅkaṇam) āyiṟṟu.
uḷḷiṭattē kaṇṇik koṇṭa kuḻi eṉap piṟavaṟṟiyum oṭṭik koḷka.
akam kaṇ am eṉṉum muc col oṭṭu oru col taṉmippaṭṭu niṟpatē aṅkaṇam. ippaṭip pala coṟkaḷ oru collāy niṟkumō eṉa aṟintōr aiyuṟār;  arivāḷ maṇi muccoṟ kūṭṭu oru col. oṭṭu illāta muḻu muḻuc coṟkaḷ. avi  arital ciṟitāy aṟuttal; aritaṟkuriya vāḷ; avvāḷ amitaṟkuriya maṇi (palaki) immūṉṟu uṟuppukaḷum uṇmi kāṇka.
mukam vāy eṉpavi oru mutalum ōruṟuppumām peyarkaḷic cuṭṭuvaṉa; iraṇṭum cērntu mukavāy eṉa oru col taṉmiyāyum amiyum. vāykkuk kīḻēyuḷḷa tāṭiyik kuṟikkum pōtu mukavāyk kaṭṭi eṉa muccoṟ kūṭṭākum. mukattil vāy, vāyiṉ kaṭṭi; ivi oṉṟiṉ pakuti oṉṟāy ataṉ pakuti oṉṟāyk kuṟittali aṟika. ataṉ kocci vaṭivu mukarak kaṭṭi eṉpatām.
ātalāl, akam kaṇ am eṉap pala col iṇintu oru col ākumō eṉa aiyuṟavu vēṇṭiyatilli eṉka.

iraṇṭu tūṇkaḷukku naṭuviluḷḷa iṭam	 ve.vi.pē.

aṅkatac ceyyuḷ 
aṅkatac ceyyuḷi āciriyar tolkāppiyar kuṟippiṭukiṟār. veḷippaṭa vaci kūṟum cemporuḷ aṅkatam paḻi maṟittuk kūṟum paḻikarappu aṅkatam eṉṉum iru pakuppiyum cuṭṭukiṟār. veṇpā yāppi ataṟku urimiyum ākkukiṟār. ivvaṅkatam paḻippatu pōlap pukaḻvatākavum pukaḻvatu pōlap paḻippatākavum amintu vañcap pukaḻcci eṉṉum peyarum piṉṉāḷil peṟṟatu.
aṅkatac ceyyuḷ taṉippāṭal tokutikaḷuḷ pala uḷavēṉum, nūlāka amintatu arumiyē. avvakiyil kavimaṇi tēcika vināyakar iyaṟṟiya marumakkaḷ vaḻi māṉmiyam eṭuttukkāṭṭām pāvēntariṉ iruṇṭa vīṭum eṇṇat takkatām. āyiṉ, ivi aṅkatam eṉṉum nūl vakip peyarip peṟṟila eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
vaciyoṭum naciyoṭum puṇarntaṉ ṟāyiṉ
aṅkatac ceyyuḷ eṉmaṉār pulavar	- tol. 1386
aṅkatam eṉpatu vaci; ataṉi iruvāṟṟāl kūṟuka eṉpāṉ itu kūṟiṉāṉ. vāy kāvātu collappaṭṭa vaciyē cemporuḷ aṅkatam eṉappaṭum. vacipporuḷiṉic cemporuḷ paṭāmal icippatu paḻikarappu aṅkatam. māṟṟaracaṉiyum, avaṉ iḷaṅkōviṉiyum vaci kūṟumāṟu pōlātu tam kōṉiyum avaṉ iḷaṅkōṉiyum vaci kūṟuṅkāl tāṅki urippar; avi pōlap paḻippaṉa eṉṟavāṟu(tol. poruḷ. 437-8. pērā.)

acaṅku 
āci eṉṉum poruḷil acaṅku eṉṉum col virutunakar vaṭṭārattil vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. oṉṟi aṭintatum ataṉi viṭuttu aṭutta oṉṟaṉmēl acintu cellum āciyi acaṅku eṉṟaṉar.
acati 
ayarvu > ayati > acati.
yakaram cakaramākat tirital makkaḷ vaḻakku.
uyaram > ucaram, mayaṟki > macakki.
tacarataṉ, tacamukaṉ eṉpavaṟṟil varum tacam vaṭacol. ataṉit tamiḻākkam ceyyum kampar tayarataṉ eṉpār. atil varum cakara yakarattiripu vēṟām
acappil terital 
acivu > acippu = kaṇṇacivu, pārvi.
oru pārviyil terital. oruvarip pōlavē oruvar tōṟṟam amintiruttal uṇṭu. atilum kūrntu nōkkāmal mēlōṭṭamāka nōkkiṉāl. avarip pōlavē ivarum tōṉṟuvār. attakiyari avarāka niṉittu ētō colla vāyeṭuppar; colliyum viṭuvar. avarkkō ivar yāreṉat teriyāmal tikippuṇṭām. anniliyi aṟintu ivar vēṟoruvar eṉpati, acappilē pārttāl eṉ naṇpar iṉṉārip pōlat tōṟṟam iruntatu. avareṉṟē niṉittuk koṇṭēṉ; poṟuttuk koḷḷuṅkaḷeṉpar. itil acappil eṉpatu oru pārviyil (oru tōṟṟattil) eṉṉum poruḷ taruvataṟika.
acalam 
calam = acivatu, ōṭuvatu, nīr.
al + calam = aciyātatu, ōṭātatu; mali.
vēṅkaṭa acalam, maruta acalam eṉpavi vēṅkaṭācalam, marutācalam āyami vaṭamoḻip puṇarccit tirippu.
aḷakka lākā aḷavum poruḷum
tuḷakka lākā niliyum tōṟṟamum
vaṟappiṉum vaḷantarum vaṇmiyum malikkē	- naṉ. 28
* calam kāṇka.
acā 
acā = tuṉpam, ayarvu > ayā > acā.
talivikkaminta tuṇiyuḷ oṉṟu acāttuṇi
tuṉpattiṟku yārē tuṇiyāvār	avar, acāttuṇi.
nalaṅkēḻ arivi pulampacā viṭavē	- kuṟun. 238
acukaṇi 
acuku + aṇi = acukaṇi.
mocci avari payaṟu ākiya ceṭikaḷil uṇṭākip pallāyiram pūccikaḷ toṭuttuc carampōl amintu ceṭiyiṉ uratti uṟuñci, viḷivik keṭukkum oruvaki nuṇpuḻu acukaṇi, acuku + aṇi, ciṟupūcci varici. kipaṭṭāl picupicu eṉṟu aruvaṟuppāka iruppatu. koḻumi mikka ceṭikaḷil paṟṟik kiṭakkum appūcci paṟṟāmal iruppataṟku muṟkāppāka maruntukaḷ teḷippatik kaṇṭuḷar. picupicukkum acukuṇiyiṉ aṭicaliyum toṭuppiyum uṇarntavar. aḻukaṇi eṉavum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. nacukaṇi eṉpārum uṇṭu.
acuṇam 
al + cuṇam = acuṇam.
acuṇamā eṉpatāl vilaṅku vakiyic cērntatu aḵtu ākum. mellici tavaḻuṅkāl avvicikku mayaṅki icippāri aṇukiya aḷavil avar vallici eḻuppi ataṉ cevippaṟi kiḻiya vīḻntu paṭac ceyvar eṉṟum ataṉi vēṭṭiyāṭip paṟṟu muṟi aḵteṉṟum nūlkaḷālum urikaḷālum aṟiya varuvaṉa.
evvilaṅkukkum eppaṟavikkum vāyāta ceviyuṇarvu itaṟku amintatu eṉpatu viyappām. im melluṇarvē ivaṟṟiṉ aḻivukku mūlam pōlum. mācuṇam eṉpatu pāmpu. ataṟku aravam eṉpatu peyar. aravam melloliyām. pūcuṇi eṉṉum koṭivaki aṟivōm. pūvoṭu kūṭiya cuṇi toṭṭār viravil oṭṭik kaḻuvutaṟkum aritāy irukkum. ataṉāl ataṉiyē akkoṭikkup peyarāka iṭṭaṉar. atupōl maṟṟavaṟṟukku illā uṇarvu kaṇṭu iṭappaṭṭa peyar acuṇamām.
koḷḷutaṟkuk kūṭāta - allāta, cuṇappiṉi - uṇarviṉi - yuṭiyatu acuṇamām.
acumpu 
acumpu = nīrkkacivu. palvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷil uṇṭākum. nīrkkacivu ciṟṟūṟṟāy - ciṟu cuṉiyāy - ākum. ataṉ tōṟṟam acumpu ākum. akkacivup pakuti, nīrp patam uṭiyatāy - kālvittāl taṭam pativatāy - irukkum.
vār acumpu oḻuku muṉṟil			- puṟam. 114
itaṉ poruḷ:   vārnta matuc cēṟueṉpatu paḻiya uri.
* tacumpu kāṇka.
aci 
aci 1
nāvi acittu ici uṇṭākkuvatāl aci eṉappaṭṭatu. atu cīriṉ uṟuppu, acittu ici kōṭaliṉ aciyē	(yā.kā. 1. mēṟ.)
aci 2
vīṭṭu mukaṭṭil iruntu toṅku toṭari allatu kayiṟṟil kaṭṭappaṭṭu acintāṭum palaki aciyām. ūñcal vēṟu  aci vēṟu. aciyil paṭukki taliyaṇi mutaliya vaṟṟi vippatu vaḻakkam. mukavi, nelli, vaḻakku itu.
aci 3
ūcal āṭṭam eṉṉum ūcal vari pāṭi ōṭittaḷḷi āṭa viṭum iciyāṭṭattiṟkum aciyē karuviyām.
e-ṭu 
cilappatikāram   ūcal vari( 29  23-25)
aci 4
kaṭikiyārattil noṭip poḻutu kāṭṭum arivaṭṭac cuḻaliyi aci eṉpatu uṇṭu.
aci 5
ēval  acikka -āṭṭuka ; acika - uṇka eṉpavi avi.
aci 6
kūriya aṟivāḷaṉ pārviyil ulakiyakkam, acitalum icitalumoruṅkē koṇṭatākap pulappaṭṭatu.
oṉṟaṉ iruppum iyakkamum vāḻvum acitalum icitalumillāmal muṭiyātu eṉpatu teḷivāyiṟṟu. piṟanta kuḻanti iyakkattāl acivu veḷippaṭukiṟatu. veḷippaṭṭa pōtē kālik kiyi acikkat toṭaṅkukiṟatu. akkuḻanti acittal illāmal uḷḷuṟuppukaḷ aciyavum iciyavum ākiṉṟaṉa. nāvi acikkiṟatu;  tēṉ tuḷipaṭṭatum acittal veḷippaṭap pulappaṭukiṉṟatu.
acital, iyakkac cāṉṟu! acivilā nili eṉṉum tōṟṟamum acivilāmi aṉṟu. iṭiyilāp pēracivē acivilāt tōṟṟam vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. niliyilā niliyē aṉittu aṇṭaṅkaḷumām! nām irukkum nilamum, kuṭiyirukkum niliyamum ellāmum ellāmum acintum aciyāt tōṟṟaṅkaḷēyākum.
aciyiṉ nalam iciyilēyē ciṟakkiṉṟatu. pūtaṅkaḷ aṉittum acivaṉavē. aciyē acivu. iyakkam, uyirppu, aciyā nili eṉpatum acintum aciyā niliyēyām.
taṟiyil, pāṭal aci pōlavē aci uṇṭē! kāṟṟacitāṉē mūccaci! pēccaci! acika! icika!
aciccīr 
pāṭalil cila iṭaṅkaḷil aciyē cīrāka niṟpatu uṇṭu. ataṉi aciccīr eṉpar. veṇpāviṉ iṟutiyil nāḷ malar eṉṉum vāypāṭṭil varuvaṉa aciccīrām. nāḷ eṉpatu nēr aci. malar eṉpatu niriyaci.
nilamici nīṭuvāḻ vār	- tiruk. 3
yāṇṭum iṭumpi ila	- tiruk. 4
aciccol 
peyarccolloṭum viṉiccolloṭum cārttic collappaṭum col aciccol. ataṟkeṉa vēṟu poruḷ illi eṉṟu kūṟuvar. āṉāl ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟittaṉavē eṉṉum tollāciriyaṉ āṇiyi eṇṇiṉ avvāṟu poruḷ illi eṉṟu taḷḷa muṭiyātām.
kol eṉpatōr aciccol. ataṉ poruḷ kollē aiyameṉak kūṟappaṭum. (tol. 753).
aṇaṅkukol āymayil kollō kaṇaṅkuḻi
mātarkol mālumeṉ neñcu	- tiruk. 1081
eṉpatil varum collākiya kol aiyap poruḷ taravilliyā?
acittal 
mēlum kīḻum allatu iṭamum valamum acittal aciyākum. āṭu māṭukaḷ aci pōṭṭu uṇṇal aci pōṭal  aciyiṭal eṉappeṟum. aci pōṭṭut tiṉṉum āvit talimāṇākkarkku uvamippaṭuttum naṉṉūl (38). pul kaṇṭa iṭattut tiṉṟu, nīr kaṇṭa iṭattuk kuṭittu, niḻal kaṇṭa iṭattup paṭuttu, aci pōṭuvatu āviṉ iyalpām. tiṉṟa uṇavi mīṇṭum vāykkuk koṇṭu vantu meṉṟu uḷḷē iṟakkutali aci mīṭṭal eṉpar. mīṭṭum mellutali aci veṭṭal eṉpar. nañciṉi acivu ceytavaṉ eṉpatu tēvāram (583 3)
āṭu māṭukaḷip pōl cilar eppoḻutum etiyāvatu meṉṟu koṇṭē iruppar. avarkaḷiṭam eṉṉa aci pōṭukiṟīrkaḷā? eṉṉa pañcamāṉālum uṅkaḷukku aci pōṭal nillātueṉpatuṇṭu.
iṉi, eṉṇutali aci pōṭutal eṉpatu aṟivāḷar vaḻakkam. mīḷa mīḷak koṇṭu vantum puraṭṭiyum māṟṟiyum eṇṇutal aciyiṭuvatu pōṉṟatākaliṉ apporuḷukkum āyiṟṟu.
accacal 
accu acal; accaṭittatu oṉṟip pōl oṉṟu irukkum. atupōl vēṟupāṭu kāṇamuṭiyāta oppāṉa amippu accacal eṉappaṭum. itu nelli vaḻakkuc col.

ivar avarip pōla accacalāka irukkiṟār, ituvum atuvum accacal tām


accaṭiyāḷam 
acciṭṭuc ceytāṟ pōṉṟa aṭiyāḷam. accacal eṉpatum atu. aṭimi eṉpataṉ aṭiyāḷamākap pacci kuttutal mutalākac ceyyappaṭṭa aṭiyāḷamām.
aṭimi eṉpatik kāṭṭum cūṭṭukkuṟieṉṟum kūṟuvar. (ce.co.a.mu.)
accam 
tummal oli accu, accu eṉa veḷippaṭum. tummal ēṟpaṭṭāl tuyara aṭippaṭi eṉpatāl nūṟu eṉpar. mīṇṭum tummiṉāl irunūṟu eṉpar. nūṟāṇṭu nōy noṭi iṉṟi vāḻka. irunūṟāṇṭu nōynoṭi iṉṟi vāḻka eṉpataṉ aṭiyāḷam avvāḻttām. tummal tuyar aṟikuṟi eṉak karutappaṭṭatāl,
tummuc ceṟuppa aḻutāḷ
aḻittaḻutāḷ
eṉṟatu kuṟaḷ (1318, 1317)
accu eṉṉum tummal oli vaḻiyē uṇṭākiya accam meyppāṭukaḷuḷ oṉṟāyiṟṟām. nacceṉṟu tummiṉāṉ eṉpatum accavaḻip paṭṭatēyām.
aṇaṅkē vilaṅkē kaḷvartam iṟiyeṉap

accamikkārip peṟutaliṉ peṟāmiyē naṉṟu eṉṟatu naṟuntoki (4). añci añcic cāvāreṉṟār pāratiyār.

accalatti 
accu + alatti = accattāl alamaral ceyvatu.
accatti uṇṭākkum pēccum, ceyalum nēruṅkāl accalattiyāka uḷḷatu eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
aṉpiṉāl ceyyum maṉattuyaram iccalatti eṉappaṭum.
icci + alatti = iccalatti.
accaṉ 
kuṭanāṭṭār tantiyi accaṉ eṉpar eṉpatu naṉṉūl uri (273)
appaṉ, attaṉ, accaṉ eṉpavi mūṉṟum tantiyik kuṟikkum collē. appā nāṉ vēṇṭutal kēṭṭaruḷ purital vēṇṭum eṉṉum vaḷḷalār, attā accā eṉpavaṟṟi yum vaḻaṅkuvār (tiruvaruṭpā).
uṇarvuk kuṟikaḷāka varum coṟkaḷ uṟavup peyarkaḷāka amitalē mikuti. appā, ammā, appappā, ammammā eṉappala vaḻaṅkum. avvāṟē accā, accō, accaccō, accāvō eṉa vaḻaṅkutal tamiḻakam taḻuviya vaḻakkēyām.
accāṇi 
accu + āṇi = accāṇi
uruḷperuntērk(ku) accāṇi 	- tiruk. 667
accu = uruḷ kōtta maram; āṇi = uruḷ kaḻalātu ataṉ kaṭikkaṇ cerukuvatu. atu vaṭivāl ciṟitāy iruntē periya pārattik koṇṭuykkum tiṭpam uṭittu. (parimē. uri)
accāṟu 
ūṟukāy eṉpati accāṟu eṉpatu tañci vaḻakku. cāṟu, paḻam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ piḻivu. miḷakuccāṟu, puḷiccāṟu eṉpatuṭaṉ cāṟu eṉpatum potuvaḻakkuc collē. iccāṟu uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ vittup payaṉpaṭuttuvatu. ataṉiṉum vēṟupaṭṭatu al cāṟu > accāṟu, neṭunāḷ ūṟavittup payaṉ koḷvatu.
o.nō. 
nal ceḷḷi > nacceḷḷi
acci 
accaṉ eṉṉum āṇpālukkuriya peṇpāl acci eṉpatu. acci peṇpāl iṟutiyāka (īṟāka) iruppatuṭaṉ (tamiḻacci). ammā eṉṟum, talivi eṉṟum poruḷ tarum. atu kātaliyik kuṟippatākak kumari māvaṭṭattil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
acciram 
acciram = paṉi. al + ciram = aṟciram > acciram.

appaṟavi kuttuvatupōl kuttum paṉi acciram ākum. acciram varuttum ūr accirappākkam.

accu 
vaṇṭi acciṉiyō, acciṭuvatiyō kuṟiyāta accu eṉa oṉṟu potumakkaḷ vaḻakkil uṇṭu. accaṭic cīli (cēli)eṉpatu, cuṅkaṭic cīli. neṟpayir naṭukikku muṉ camaṉiliyāy - ōram cāram veṭṭi oḻuṅkuṟṟatāy - nilattip paṇpaṭuttutali accuttiraṭṭal eṉpatu uḻavar vaḻakku. accu eṉpatu vārppaṭak kācu eṉṉum poruḷil kalveṭṭu uṇṭu.
accup paṇi nāḷ nāḷum ciṟakkiṉṟatu. accakaṅkaḷ perukukiṉṟaṉa eḻuttup piḻikaḷum avaṟṟi nīkkap paṭumpāṭum, accup paṇiyō naccup paṇiyō- eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyi uṇṭākkiyuḷḷatu. uruḷ ūrti vaḻiyē vanta accu, ulakattēr accāka māṟiya māṭci viyappāṉatu.
accoṭital 
peruñcētam; pēriḻappu ēṟpaṭal. etirpārā vakiyil cotti ellām iḻakkum nilimi ēṟpaṭṭu viṭṭāl, acci oṭittu viṭṭatu eṉṟō accoṭintuviṭṭatu eṉṟō colvatu vaḻakkam.
vaṇṭiyil uḷḷa ōr uṟuppu accu; avvaccu, tiraṇṭa irumpāl āṉatu. atu, oṭintuviṭṭāl oṭinta iṭatti viṭṭu vaṇṭi nakarātu. atupōl oruvar ceyaloḻintu pōkumāṟu tiṭumeṉṟu ēṟpaṭum poruḷ iḻappu ac coṭital eṉappaṭum.
accō

accaṉ = appaṉ, accō eṉa viḷittu aravaṇikkum piḷḷit tamiḻp paruvaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. accōp pattueṉpatu atu.

añar 
ayar > añar.
ayarvu = cōrvu; ayarttal = maṟattal.
ayarvu maṟati iṇiccol
kaṭiya ōṭṭam, oyā uḻippu! ivaṟṟāl ayarvu uṇṭākiṉṟatu! iyakkattāl tēymāṉam ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu ōṭṭamum uḻippum tanta ayarvi īṭu ceyyāviṭiṉ kēṭām, uṭalil uṇṭām cōrvu, eṇṇum eṇṇattiyum taṭukkiṉṟatu. ayarttu maṟantu pōkiṉṟatu. ayarttal eṉpatu maṟatip poruḷ tarukiṉṟatu. ayattup pōccueṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku. ayarvum añarvum oru poruḷavē.
añar = tuṉpam, cōrvāl uṇṭām tuṉpam.
kaṭumpuṉal tokutta naṭuṅkañar aḷḷal		- kuṟun. 103
añcaṭittal 
aintu > añcu + aṭittal = añcaṭittal
toḻil paṭuttu viṭutal.
avar kaṭi añcaṭikkiṟatueṉṟāl, īyōṭṭukiṟār eṉpatu pōṉṟa vaḻakkākum. kaṭiyil vaṇikam nikaḻavilli eṉpatu poruḷ, toḻil cīrāka illi añcaṭittu viṭṭatueṉpatum kēṭkak kūṭiyatu. añcu eṉpatu aintu eṉṉum poruḷatu. iṅku aintu mey vāy kaṇ mūkku ceviyākiya aintu uṟuppukaḷiyuṭiya uṭalik kuṟittu niṟkiṟatu.
aṭittup pōṭṭa uṭal aciyāmal kiṭappatu pōlak kaṭi vaṇikamum, toḻil iyakkamum paṭuttuviṭṭaṉa eṉpatu viḷakkap poruḷām. cuṟucuṟuppu illātavaṉip pārttum eṉṉa añcaṭittup pōy irukkiṟāy eṉpatum uṇṭu. itu ipporuḷi mēlum teḷivākkum.
añcalikki 
kaṇṭīrak kōp perunaṟ kiḷḷi eṉpāṉik kaṇṭa pulavar vaṉparaṇar avaṉ tōṟṟam kaṇṭavarāyt toḻutu koṇṭu eḻuntār. avaṉ taṉ kiyik kavittu irukkac coṉṉāṉ. itil varum toḻutal eṉpataṟku añcali paṇṇal eṉkiṟār puṟanāṉūṟṟu uriyāḷar. ki kuvittal, añcali paṇṇal eṉa itaṉāl aṟiyalām.
cilappatikārattil varum añcalikki eṉpataṟku,
añcali eṉpa taṟivuṟak kiḷappiṉ
eñca liṉṟi irukiyum patākiyāy
vantakam poruntu māṭcit teṉṟaṉar
antamil kāṭci aṟintici ṉōrē
eṉpati mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭukiṟār aṭiyārkku nallār. irukiyum iṇintu kūppuki atuvām;  kūttuk kali muttiriyuḷ oṉṟu. atu, kuvittal (kumpiṭutal)
añcal 
maṭal, kaṭitam, ōli, mutalāka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa col āṅkilar āṭciyil añcal eṉappaṭṭatu. añcal = kaṭitam, añcalar, añcalakam, añcal tuṟi, añcal vaṇṭi, añcal peṭṭi, añcal aṭṭi, añcal tali, añcal uṟi, añcal celavu eṉa añcal vaḻic coṟkaḷ perukalāyiṉa. añcal eṉṟa āṭci evvāṟu ēṟpaṭṭatu?
neṭiya kōl, ataṉ ucciyil vēlkatti, calacala eṉap peruka olikkum calaṅki - iviyaminta oru karuviyit tūkkik koṇṭu oruvar ōṭṭam pōṭṭu vantāl, etirē varupavar pārppavar añci otuṅkuvarā? etirē niṟparā?
avvāḷum, aracāṇiyāl kutiri mēl ēṟi virintu cellum āḷ eṉṟāl oli kēṭṭatum appālāvārā māṭṭārā? akkutiri añcal kutiri, ataṉ mēl iruppavar añcalkārar! avar kiyil piyil koṇṭa kaṭitaṅkaḷ añcalkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. añcal kutiri cila kuṟitta iṭaṅkaḷukku vantu koṭuttuc cellum kaṭitam ciṟṟūrkaḷukku ōṭunar vaḻiyākak koṇṭu cērkkappaṭum. avarum kiyil olikkum maṇiyuṭiya vēlkampu uṭiyarāka ōṭuvār. ivaṟṟāl añcal eṉpatu accattuṭaṉ virivup poruḷum taruvatāyiṟṟu.
ōrañcalil vantu viṭukiṟēṉeṉṟāl, virintu varuvēṉ eṉpatām.
innāḷil tīyaṇippu vaṇṭi, mutalutavi vaṇṭi, āṭciyar vaṇṭi eṉpavi oli kēṭṭu otuṅka vikkum ōr utti koṇṭiruttali oppiṭṭup pārkka.
ōriṭattuc ceyti maṟṟōriṭam cērac ceyvatām añcal pōl vāṉoli tolikkāṭci āyavaṟṟil pativu ceyyappaṭṭa oli, oḷik kāṭcikaḷi vēṟiṭattuk kēṭkavum kāṇavum parappuvatu innāḷil añcal eṉappaṭṭatu. añcal vaḻik kalviyum uṇṭāyatu.
añcaṉam 
ai = aḻaku. caṉṉam > caṉam. caṉṉam = ciṟitu.
aiñcaṉam > añcaṉam. aḻakiyatāy - ciṟukīṟṟāy - amintatu añcaṉam ākum. kaṇmi eṉpatu añcaṉam eṉa vaḻaṅkum. kaṇ imiyil melliya ciṟiya kīṟṟāyt taṭavi oppaṉi ceyya utavuvatu añcaṉam eṉappaṭṭatu. paṇṭē añcaṉam eḻutappaṭṭami.
eḻutuṅkāl kōlkāṇāk kaṇṇēpōl
eṉṉum vaḷḷuvattāl (1285) pulappaṭum.
añcaṉam karuniṟamātal, añcaṉa vaṇṇaṉeṉ āruyir nāyakaṉ eṉṉum kampar vākkāl (ayō. 1006) aṟiyalām.
añcaṉat tiraḷum aṇiari tāramum eṉpatu cilappatikāram (25 40).
añci 
atiyamāṉ kuṭiyiṉar tam pōrāṟṟal mēmpāṭṭāl koṇṭa peyar añci eṉpatām.
pōraṭu tiruvil polantār añci	- puṟam. 91
iṉṉāy perumaniṉ oṉṉā tōrkkē	- puṟam. 94
uṭaṉṟōr uytal yāvatu?	- puṟam. 97
nīyē kūṟṟat taṉiyi	- puṟam. 98
eṉpavi auviyār vaḻaṅkiya vīṟumoḻikaḷ. pakiyi añcac ceyvāṉ añci eṉappaṭṭāṉ. ulaki oṭukka vallāṉ oṭuṅki eṉṟu meykaṇṭārāl cuṭṭappaṭṭami aṟika (civañāṉapō. 1)
aññi 
ammi > aññi; iṉa eḻuttut tiripu. aṉṉi eṉpatum atu.
aññi nī ēṅki aḻal	- cilap. 3
aṉṉi nī
eṉpatum itaṟkup pāṭam.
ō.nō. 
mintaṉ > mañcaṉ
mañcaṉkaḻal nakukiṉṟatu kaṇṭāḷ	- kampa. ayōt. 627
aṭakkam 
aṭakkam: 1
aṭu > aṭal > aṭakkal > aṭakkam.
poṟikaḷ tattam pōkkil pōkāmal maṉavaliyāl amittu nerukkic, cellum vaḻiyāl cellac ceyvatu aṭakkamām.
ceṉṟa iṭattāl celaviṭātu tītorīi	
naṉṟiṉpāl uyppa taṟivu	- tiruk. 422
eṉṟavāṟu ceyaluṟal aṭakkamām!
pōrvīrar orē kuṟiyāka iruntu paki vellal pōl poṟi pulappakikaḷi vellal arumiyāl ataṉ peṟṟiyi, aṭakkam amararuḷ uykkumeṉṟār vaḷḷuvar (121). amar = pōr;  amarar = pōrvīrar.
aṭakkam eṉpatu uyarntōr muṉ aṭaṅki oḻukum oḻukkam. avi, paṇinta moḻiyum, taṇinta naṭiyum, tāṉi maṭakkalum, vāy putittalum mutalāyiṉa. (tol. poruḷ. 250. pērā.)
aṭakkamāvatu piṟar taṉṉi viyantu urikkat tāṉ aṭaṅki iruttal. (nālaṭi. 170. tarumar.)
aṭakkam: 2
aṭakkam ceyyappaṭṭa iṭam.
mūccu aṭaṅki appaṭiyē iyakkamaṟac ceyvatu aṭakkam eṉappaṭum. mūccup payiṟciyil tērnta ōkiyar tam mūcci iyaṅkāmal aṭakki iyaṟki eytiviṭaluṇṭu. attakiya nili aṭakkam eṉappaṭum. iḵtu iṉṉār aṭakkamāṉa iṭam eṉpatu vaḻakkāṟu.
aimpulaṉkaḷi aṭakkum aṭakkattil iruntu, mūcciyē aṭakki niṟutti viṭum ivvaṭakkattiṟkup poruḷ virivākiyatu. aṭakkam ceytal eṉap putittalik kūṟum vaḻakkuḷḷati aṟika. oṭukkam eṉpatum kāṇka!
aṭakkam oṭukkam 
aṭakkam	= 	mey vāy kaṇ mūkku cevi eṉṉum aimpoṟikaḷiyum maṉattiyum aṭakkutal
oṭukkam 	= 	paṇivuṭaṉ oṭuṅki niṟkul
orumiyuḷ āmipōl aintaṭakkal(tiruk. 126), pulaṉintum veṉṟāṉtaṉ vīramē vīramām(auvi taṉip.) eṉpavi aṭakkam.
vantavaḷ taṉṉic ceṉṉi maṇṇuṟa vaṇaṅki vācac
cinturap pavaḷac cevvāy ceṅkiyiṉ putittu maṟṟic
cuntarat taṭakkit tāṉi maṭakkuṟat tuvaṇṭu niṉṟāṉ- kampa. ayō. 291
itu kikēyi muṉṉar irāmaṉ niṉṟa paṇivu nili iḵtu oṭukkam. cāṉṟōr oṭuṅkiya iṭam oṭukkam eṉap peṟutaliyum, iṟivaṉ oṭuṅki eṉap peṟutaliyum karutuka.
aṭam piṭittal 
aṭampiṭittal eṉpatu coṉṉatik kēḷāmal, tāṉ colliyatic collic colli muraṇṭu piṭittal aṭam piṭittal ākum. kalaṅkaḷil piṭikkum kari aḻukku ākiyavi. akalāmal paṟṟik koḷḷuvati aṭṭup piṭittal eṉpar aṭṭup piṭittal pōl taṉ eṇṇatti viṭātu paṟṟutal aṭam ākum. itu nelli, mukavi vaḻakku.
aṭar 
aṭar: 1
aṭar = ceṟivu, ceṟivākac ceyyappaṭṭa takaṭu,
aṭarpukarc ciṟukaṇ yāṉi	- puṟam. 6
aḻalpurinta aṭartāmari	- puṟam. 29
aṭu >ml®
aṭar: 2
aṭar > aṭarttal
aṭarttal= aṭuttuc ceṉṟu aṭakkutal;
veḷḷi aṭarttāṟkē	- cilap. 7 9,10,12
aṭal 
aṭal = valimi, pōriṭal, vellutal, kollutal, aṭutal eṉpatum itu.
nerukkamāka irunta iruvar annerukkam kuṟiyiṉ pakiyām. pakimip paṭṭār. valimi kāṭṭavum vellavum vēṇṭip pōriṭalum aḻittalum naṭipeṟuvaṉa. ātalāl, aṭal eṉpataṟku it toṭarccip poruḷkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa.
aṭutal ākiya camittal, uṇṇaṟkut takap poruḷi ākkalām. uṇṇa vārāp poruḷkaḷi uṇṇum vakiyil pōkkuva pōkki, cērppavi cērttu ākkuva ākkalāl avaṟṟiṉ iyalpi māṟṟiyum, aḻittum ākkuvatu aṭutal, aṭal āyiṟṟām. cōṟu aṭupavari aṭavi eṉpatu yāḻppāṇa vaḻakku.
aṭavi 
aṭarnta kāṭāka uḷḷa iṭam aṭavi. aṭarvi > aṭavi. aṭartti eṉpatu ceṟivu. iruṅkāṭu, karuṅkāṭu eṉpaṉavum ceṟivu vaḻippaṭṭa coṟkaḷē.
e-ṭu: 
aṭavik katalip pacuṅkurutti	- antakak. taṉip
payir aṭarttiyāka uḷḷatu
kaḷi aṭicalāka uḷḷatu
eṉpaṉa makkaḷ vaḻakku.
aṭaviyār 
aṭaviyār eṉpatoru kuṭippirivu; appiriviṉar, oru kālattuk kuṟiñci, mulli nila vāṇarāka iruntaṉar eṉpataṟkuriya cāṉṟu atu. avarkaḷ itukāl marutanila vāṇarāka uḷar, cēṟṟūr eṉṉum mukavi māvaṭṭa ūril, aṭaviyār teru eṉa oṉṟuṇṭu.
aṭaviyāṉ 
aṭavi eṉpatu kāṭu, aṭarntu - ceṟintu - viḷaṅkutāl aṭavi āyiṟṟu. veḷippaṭāmal vīṭṭuḷ aṭaṅkic ceṟintu kiṭappavaṉi aṭaviyāṉ eṉpatu vēlūr vaḻakku.
aṭā, aṭē 
aṭuttirukkum iḷiñariyum aṭippaṭṭa uṟavu urimi uṭiyāriyum aṭā, aṭē, ēṭā (ē aṭā) ēṭē (ē aṭē) eṉa urimi yaṉpāl periyavar viḷippatu vaḻakkam.
avvāṟē peṇmakāri aṭi, aṭī, ēṭi (ē aṭi) ēṭī (ē aṭī) ēṭṭi eṉa makaḷir viḷippatum vaḻakkam. itaṉi vacic collākac colvārum kēṭpārum eṇṇuvatu illi. āṉāl urimi uṟavu illāri aṭē, aṭi, eṉil iḻimic collāy eṇṇappaṭutal āyiṟṟu.
vāaṭā, pōaṭā (vāṭā, pōṭā)
vāaṭi, pō aṭi (vāṭi, pōṭi)
eṉpavi ēval. makiḻvuk kuṟippumām.
veḷḷip paṉimaliyiṉ mītulāvuvōm - aṭi
	mēlik kaṭalmuḻutum kappal viṭuvōm	- pāratiyār
aṭaṭē(aṭē aṭē) - viyappukkuṟi.
aṭaṭē appaṭiyā āyiṟṟu?	
* ēlē kāṇka.
aṭāvaṭi 
aṭu + ā + aṭi= aṭāvaṭi
aṭu = poruntutal; aṭā = poruntāmi.
pōr eṉpatu oppup poruḷatu. poruntalāl poru. pōr eṉappaṭuvatu. atu poruntā vakiyil muṟikēṭām vakiyil oruvaṉāl ceyyappaṭumāṉāl, aṭāvaṭi eṉṟum, aṭāvaṭikkāraṉ eṉṟum aṭāvaṭittaṉam eṉṟum makkaḷāl collappaṭukiṉṟatu. pōraṟam aṅkē pōṟṟappaṭavilli eṉpatām.
aṭi 
aṭi1
ōr uruviṉ aṭippakuti aṭiyām. aṭiyām aṭippakuti kāl ākum. māntar kāliṉ aḷavu aṭi eṉappaṭṭu aḷavip peyar āyiṟṟu; atu. nīṭṭalaḷavi. kāliṉ aḷavukoṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭa aḷavum aṭi eṉappaṭṭatu. ataṉi aḷakkum kōl eṉṟār vaḷḷuvar (710) vaḻakkil aḷavu kōl, aṭikkucci eṉa uḷḷaṉa. aṭi eṉpatu muṉpu paṉṉiraṇṭu viral (aṅkula) aḷavākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. itukāl patiṉma muṟiyil māttam (mīṭṭar) eṉappaṭukiṟatu.
aṭi2
oṉṟaṉ muṭippakuti muṭiyām. aṭimuṭi iraṇṭum oṉṟaṉ kīḻelli mēlelliyām. muṭiyāvatu muṭivu iṭam. ōr uru allatu poruḷ nilatti aṭuttut toṭṭirukkum pakuti aṭi ākum. aṭu > aṭi. nilattaṭi nīr, nilattiṉ uḷḷē iruppatu. marattaṭi mutaliyaṉa nilattiṉ uḷḷiruntu kiḷarntu nilattiṉ mēl eḻuntu niṟpaṉa. iyaṅkuvaṉavaṟṟiṉ kālkaḷ nilatti aṭuttu iruppatāl aṭi eṉappaṭṭaṉa. aṭimaram, aṭimuṭi, puḷḷaṭi, makkaḷ, maṟṟi uyirikaḷil kāl amintavi ellāvaṟṟukkum kāl aṭiyē aṭi, maraṅkaḷ mutalām ōraṟi vuyirikaḷukku maṇmēl teriyum pakuti aṭiyum, avaṟṟiṉ vēriṉ kīḻppakuti vērum vēraṭiyumām.
oṉṟi aṭuttatāl - neruṅkiyatāl - ēṟpaṭṭa ōci aṭi eṉalāyatu. kōlāl tākkutalum, kiyāl aṟitalum, aṭi eṉa ēvalum āyatu.
vantaṭi porunti munti niṟpiṉ	- puṟam. 10
ivvaṭi kālām aṭiyām.
aṭikkaṇ taṅkutal aṭiyuṟi eṉappaṭṭatu.
aṭiniḻaṟ paḻakiya aṭiyuṟi	- puṟam. 198
viṭutal iṉṟi oṉṟiyiruttal aṭippaṭal ākum. oru payir viḷintu, ataṉ aṭip pakutiyil mīḷa eḻunta payir tāḷaṭi eṉappaṭum. palkāl eṉpatu aṭikkaṭiyām.
aṭi eṉpatu aṭā aṭē eṉpatu pōla, aṭī eṉa viḷiyāyiṟṟu. kalaṅkaḷil paṟṟup piṭittal aṭippiṭittal eṉappaṭṭatu. aṭiyip paṟṟutal vaḻipāṭum, maṉṟāṭṭum, tōlvik kuṟiyum, aṭimic cāṉṟum āyiṉa.
aṭiyēṉ eṉpatu aṭiyavar paṇimoḻi, marattaṭi, kaḷattaṭi, ūraṭi mutaliyaṉa iṭappeyar cuṭṭiṉa. aṭittal toḻiṟpeyar. katir aṭittal, kāyaṭittal, ēraṭittal, parampaṭittal, paṇṇiyaṭittal, puḻutiyaṭittal, pēyaṭittal, māraṭittal, araṭṭi aṭittal mutaliyaṉa peruvaḻakkāṉavi. iyaṟki iyaṅkiyal veyilaṭittal, kāṟṟaṭittal maḻiyaṭittal eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
vayiṟṟil aṭittal, paṭṭuṇi pōṭutal eṉalāyiṟṟu. aintaṭittal eṉpatu añcaṭittal eṉṟu vaḻu vaḻakkāyatu. aimpoṟikaḷum aṭittup pōṭṭatu pōl ceyalaṟṟuk kiṭattal aintaṭittalām.
ciṅkiyaṭittal eṉpatu uṇavukkut tiṇṭāṭutal ākum.
ōr aṭip pakutiyil mēl koṇṭu āytal, aṭippaṭi āyvu neṟiyām. aṭiyiṉ camappāṭu, aṭimaṭṭam, parantupaṭṭuc cellum ōciyi uṭiya pāṭal naṭiyiṭa amintatu aṭi. nāṟcīr aḷavaṭi; muccīr cintaṭi; irucīr kuṟaḷaṭi oṉṟi mutaṟkaṇ eḻutum ōli, aṭiyōli; ataṉip paṭi eṭuppatu paṭiyōli; aṭiyōli mūla ōliyām.
aṭipaṭuvatāl uṇṭāvatu kāyam . vaṭu. atu māṟātu. nāvaṭiyō aṭiyāmal aṭittu aḻiyā  vaṭuvāvatu.
aṭikoṭi 
aṭi eṉpatu vēr;  koṭi eṉpatu vēril iruntu taḷirttup paṭarum koṭi koṭiyāvatu aṭiyiṉ muṭi. antāti eṉpati aṭikoṭi eṉpatu yāḻppāṇa vaḻakku. iḵtu iyaṟkiyoṭum iṇinta iṉiya amippiṉatu.
aṭicāl piṭicāl 
aṭicāl 	= 	viti teḷippataṟkāka aṭikkum cāl
piṭicāl 	= 	teḷitta vitiyi mūṭuvataṟkāka aṭikkum cāl
cālāvatu āḻamākavum, akalakamāvum uḻuvatu. aṭicāl, piṭicāl vēḷāṇmit toḻil vaḻiyāka vaḻaṅkappeṟum iṇiccol. cāl eṉpatu uḻupaṭic cāl eṉpataṉ curukkam. uḻupaṭiyāvatu kalappi. mēl maṇṇum kīḻmaṇṇum kalakkavikkum kalappi, potu nili uḻavu viṭuttu, āḻa uḻutal cāl aṭittal eṉappaṭum. cālaṭittal viti teḷippataṟkum pātti kaṭṭuvataṟkum iṭaṉāka amiyum uḻu taṭattil viti pōṭutal cāl viti eṉappaṭum.
aṭicil 
aṭucil > aṭicil. aṭutal  camittal
aṭṭu - camittup - pakkuvappaṭuttuvatu aṭicil ākum.
aṭicilum pūvum	- tol. 109
ūṉtuvi aṭicil	- puṟam. 390
neyyuṭi aṭicil	-puṟam. 188
uṇporuḷkaḷil ākāvaṟṟi aḻittu ākkam taruvaṉa koṇṭu ākkalāl aṭutal eṉpataṟkuc camittal poruḷum, aṭicil eṉpataṟku uṇporuḷ eṉṉum poruḷum uṇṭāyiṉavām.
muṉpē, aṭicil nūl iruntami, paṉuvaliṉ vaḻāap palvē ṟaṭicil(ciṟupāṇ. 241) eṉpatāl pulappaṭum. maṭi nūl eṉpatum itu.
* maṭinūl kāṇka.
aṭitirumpal 
aṭi tirumpum vēḷi eṉpatu kālaṭi niḻal kiḻakkil cāyum moḻutu, atāvatu ucci cāynta mālip poḻutu ākum. aṭittirumpi viṭṭatu. vīṭṭukkup pōkalāmeṉpar. aṭiyic cārnta niḻalik kuṟittu. nēram kuṟippatu itu. itu teṉ tamiḻaka vaḻakkākum.
aṭittal 
aṭittal= kiṭittal, uṇṇal, vetuppal, acittal.
aṭittal eṉpatu aṭittalām viṉiyik kuṟiyāmal paricu aṭittatu. eṉpatil kiṭittal poruḷil varukiṟatu. vayiṟu niṟiya aṭittuviṭṭēṉ.v‹gâš uṇṇaṟ poruḷ aṭittalukku uṇṭākiṉṟatu. kāyccal aṭittal pōṉṟaṉavaṟṟil aṭiyāta aṭi aṭiyākiṉṟatu. āṅku veyilaṭittal pōla veppap poruḷ tantatu. vayiṟṟil aṭittal eṉpatilō paṭṭuṇi pōṭutalik kuṟippatāyiṟṟu. etil aṭittālum vayiṟṟil aṭittalākātu. kaṇṇaṭittal eṉpatō acittal poruḷatu.
aṭittup piṭittu 
aṭittal	=	oruvaṉ kiyiyō kiyiluḷḷa maṇṇiyō taṭṭutal.
piṭittal 	= 	taṭṭiviṭṭu ōṭupavaṉit tappaviṭāmal taṭuttup piṭittal.
maṇtaṭṭi ōṭip piṭittal eṉṉum ciṟuvar viḷi yāṭṭil aṭittup piṭittu ōṭal eṉṉum iṇiccol vaḻaṅkum.
aṭipiṭi 
aṭi 	= 	aṭittal eṉpatu mutaṉili yaḷavil aṭiyeṉa niṉṟatu.
piṭi 	= 	piṭittal eṉpatum mutaṉili yaḷavil piṭiyeṉa niṉṟatu.
aṭiyum piṭiyum eṉa ummit tokiyāy amintu iṇiccollākiyatu.
caṇṭi, pōr, viḷiyāṭṭu eṉpavaṟṟil tākkuvārum taṭuppārum eṉa iru tiṟattar uḷaraṉṟō! tākkuvār viṉippāṭu aṭi, taṭuppār viṉippāṭu piṭi.
veṭci, curanti, vañci, kāñci, uḻiñi, nocci, tumpi, vāki, eṉap puṟattiṇikaḷ iru kūṟupaṭṭu amitali aṟika. ivi pōrkkaḷa aṭipiṭikaḷ.
nī yaṭittāl eṉki puḷiyaṅkāy piṭuṅkavā pōkum eṉpatu vaḻaṅku moḻi.
aṭipiṟakkiṭal 
aṭi + piṟakku + iṭal = aṭipiṟakkiṭal.
pōril niṉṟu tākka añcip piṉṉiṭal aṭipiṟakkiṭal ākum. kālip piṉvāṅkal allatu piṉvittal pōril mutuku kāṭṭalukku muṉṉōṭiyātalāl aṭipiṟakkiṭṭōrit tākkutal pōraṟam aṉṟu eṉpatu paṇṭit tamiḻar vaḻakku. (puṟam. 9 tol. puṟat. 10 nac.)
aṭiputi araṇam 
kālaṭi, paraṭu, kaṇukkāl eṉpavi vari muḻutuṟa mūṭik kākkap pōr vīrarkaḷ pūṇṭatum tōlāl āyatumām ceruppu, aṭiputi araṇam eṉappaḻanāḷil vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. putittal = mūṭutal; araṇam = kāval.
e-ṭu: 
aṭiputi araṇam	-perum. 69
* ceruppu kāṇka.
aṭippāvāṭi 
aṭi + pāvāṭi =  aṭippāvāṭi.
pāvāṭikku uḷḷāka uṭuttiya pāvāṭiyi aṭippāvāṭi (uṭpāvāṭi) eṉpatu kumari vaṭṭāra vaḻakku. aṭi maram ceṭi koṭikaḷiṉ vērum tūrumām pakuti maṇṇuḷ iruntu varuvatāl uḷ eṉṉum poruḷ koṇṭatu.
aṭippoṭi 
aṭippoṭi = toṇṭar.
aṭi = kālaṭi; poṭi = tūci, tūḷ, kālaṭiyil paṭṭa tūḷ eṉap poruḷ kuṟittālum, aṭipaṭṭa iṭattil uḷḷa maṇṇi eṭuttu, vaḻipaṭum poruḷ pōlap pūcik koḷḷuvāri aṭippoṭi eṉpar.
civaṉiyarkkup poṭi, tīrunīṟu,
māliyarkkup poṭi, tirumaṇ,
toṇṭaraṭippoṭiyāḻvār nālāyirap paṉuvalār, kampaṉ aṭippoṭi kaṇēcaṉār aṇittē vāḻntavar.
aṭimuṭi uṟuppukaḷ 
akaṅkāl ukirviral mīkāl paraṭu kaṇimuḻantāḷ
mikuṅkāl nitampamum untiyutara marimuliyum
nakañcār viralaṅki muṉkitōḷ kaṇṭam mukamnakivāy
nakuṅkā titaḻmūkkuk kaṇpuru vamneṟṟi tāḻkuḻalē	- navanītap. 44
akaval, vañcip pākkaḷāl uṟuppu (avayava) māli varum eṉavum, kalippā varutaliyum vilakkār eṉavum kūṟuvar. (poykiyār pāṭṭiyal; navanītap pāṭṭiyal 44 mēṟkōḷ)
apparaṭikaḷ oru patikattit tiruvaṅka māli yākap pāṭiṉār. atil tali, kaṇ, mūkku, vāy, neñcu, ki, ākki, kāl eṉpaṉa muṟiyē collap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. aṅku =vaḷivu. vaḷintu nimirum uṭal aṅkam āyatu.
periyāḻvār pāṭiya kaṇṇaṉ tiruppātātikēca vaṇṇattil pātam, viral, kaṇikkāl, muḻantāḷ, kuṟaṅku, muttak kācu, maruṅku, unti, utaram, mārpu, tōḷ, ki, kaṇṭam, vāy, nākku, nayaṉam, muṟuval, mūkku, kaṇkaḷ, puruvam, kuḻi, neṟṟi, kuḻal eṉpaṉa muṟiyē ōtap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
apparaṭikaḷ tam tali mutaliyavaṟṟi viḷittup pāṭiṉār. periyāḻvār kaṇṇaṉ aṭimutal muṭiyīṟākap pāṭiṉār. ivi aṅka mālikkuriya ilakkaṇattip peṟāvāyiṉum. aṅka mālikku muṉṉōṭikaḷ eṉṉattakkaṉa.
aṅka mālikkup piṉṉōṭi eṉṉat takkatum uṇṭu. atu, meymaṟi (kavacam) eṉṉum peyar koṇṭatu. vināyaka kavacam, caṭṭi kavacam mutaliyaṉa avi. iṉṉa iṉṉa uṟuppukaḷi ippaṭi itaṉāl kākkeṉavarum amivuṭi yavi avi.
teyvaṅkaḷukkup pātāti kēcamum (aṭimuṭiyum), ēṉiyavarkaḷukkuk kēcāti pātamum (muṭiyaṭiyum) kūṟutal marapeṉpareṉpatu navanītap pāṭṭiyal kuṟippuri (43)
aṭimi 
kālaṭiyic cārntu toṇṭu pūṇṭum, urimi iḻantu aṭimippaṭṭum iruppavar aṭimi eṉappaṭuvar.
kuruvar aṭimi, tuṟavar aṭimi, iṟiyaṭimi mutalām aṭimiyarkku aṭimi uyarvup poruḷum, vaṟumi iḻimikaḷāl tam urimiyaṟṟuk kiṭappārkku iḻimip poruḷum aṭimi vaḻiyē uṇṭām.
tillivāḻ antaṇartam aṭiyārkkum aṭiyēṉ
tirunīla kaṇṭattuk kuyavaṉārkkum aṭiyēṉ	- cun. tēvā.
murukaṭimi, kampaṉ aṭippoṭi, toṇṭaraṭippoṭi pōlvaṉa uyarvukkuriya aṭimi.
nāṭṭāl aṭimi, moḻiyāl aṭimi, kula aṭimi, kottaṭimi eṉpaṉa iḻimippaṭṭa aṭimi.
* aṭippoṭi kāṇka.
aṭiyōli accōli 
aṭiyōli 	= 	mutaṟkaṇ eḻutappaṭṭa ōli allatu 		mūla ōli.
accōli 	= 	mūla ōliyip pārttu eḻutappaṭṭa 		paṭiyōli.
aṭimaṉi, aṭimaṇi eṉpavaṟṟil uḷḷa aṭi mūlamātal teḷivikkum.
mūlattil uḷḷatu uḷḷapaṭi, paṭiyeṭuppatu ac cōliyām. mūṭciyil kiḻinta ōli paṭiyōli mūla ōli, māṭciyiṟ kāṭṭa vittēṉ(periya. taṭut. 36) eṉa varuvatil cuṭṭum paṭiyōli accōliyām.
accaṭic cīli, accu vellam, vārppaṭa accu eṉpa vaṟṟik koṇṭu accu paṭiyātal aṟika.
aṭivili 
aṭivili 		=	ūṉvili
māṭu viṟpataṟkākat tāmpaṇikkuk koṇṭu celvar. aṅkuk kuṟinta vilikku māṭṭik kēṭṭāl. aṭi vilikkuk kēṭkiṟāyā? vēli ceyyum māṭu itueṉpar. aṭivili eṉpatu kaṟiyiṉ viliyik kaṇakkiṭṭu ūṉ uṇavup poruḷukkāk koṇṭu cellappaṭum māṭākum. aṭivili eṉpatu māṭaṭittuk koṉṟu kūṟu pōṭṭu vilikku viṟpāriṭam koṭuttu, vāṅkum viliyākum. attaku māṭukaḷ aṭimāṭukaḷ eṉappaṭum. aṭittal  kollutal.
aṭukiṭi 
oṉṟi niṟivēṟṟik koḷḷumāṟu oruvar muṉṉō, vaḻipaṭu teyvattiṉ muṉṉō uṇṇāmalum parukāmalum uyiri viṭutaṟkut tuṇintārāyp palarum aṟiyak kiṭakkum kiṭinili aṭukiṭi eṉappaṭum.
aṭutal = aḻittal. taṉṉi oṟuttal vaḻiyākat tāṉ eṇṇiya eṇṇatti muṭittuk koḷḷal atu. piṟarkku iṉṉā ceyyāmi vaḻiyākat taṉṉi vāṭṭit taṉ eṇṇatti niṟivēṟṟalām atu.
aṭukiṭi paṭukiṭi 
aṭuttum paṭuttum kiṭattal, viṭṭup piriyāmal iruttal; oṉṟic cātikka niṉippār eppōtum oṉṟi allatu oruvaric cārntu iruttal aṭukiṭi - aṭuttuk kiṭappatu.
paṭukkiyilēyē kiṭattal - paṭukiṭi.
aṭukiṭi paṭukiṭiyāk kiṭantu peṇṇik kaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku
aṭukkal 
aṭukkal = mali; oṉṟaṉmēl oṉṟu vittatu pōl aminta mali aṭukkal ākum. aṭukkup pāṟi eṉpatoru malippeyar. aṭukkumoḻi, aṭukkuc caṭṭi, aṭukkumāṭi eṉpavi vaḻakkukaḷ aṭukku malliki, iyaṟkik koṭi.
aṭukkal mīmici aruppam pēṇātu	- mali. 19
aṭukkuvatu porunta amintatu. 
aṭukkātatu porunta amiyātatu.
nī ceyyum ceyal aṭukkātu	eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku
aṭukkal, aṭukkam eṉpatumām.
aṭukkaḷi 
aṭukku + aḷ + ai = aṭukkaḷi.
oṉṟi oṉṟu neruṅka vippatu aṭukku ākum. pakkam vittal mēlē vittal eṉa iruvaki vippum aṭukkē. kīḻē aṭuttaṭuttu amittatu aṭuppu ākum. mēlē mēlē vippatu aṭukku ākum.
e-ṭu:  
aṭukku malli, aṭukkup pāṟi, aṭukkuccaṭṭi, aṭukkup pāṉi.
coṟkaḷi aṭukkik kūṟal aṭukkumoḻi; aṭukkut toṭar. uṇavākkum maṭippaḷḷi eṉiṉum, vīṭṭuc camiyal aṟi eṉiṉum kalaṅkaḷ palavāy - ceṟivāy - vikkappaṭutalāl aḵtu aṭukkaḷi eṉappaṭṭatu. aḷ eṉpatu ceṟivu.
x.neh.:  
ceṟivaṟṟa maṇ aḷ al(aḷḷal). camiyalaṟiyi aṭukkaḷi eṉpatu vaḻakku.
aṭukku 
aṭukku:1
oṉṟi aṭuttu oṉṟu amivatum, aṭukki vippatum aṭukku ākum. aṭukkuppāṟi, aṭukku malli, aṭukkam (malittoṭar) eṉpaṉa pōlvaṉa aṭuttaṭut tamintavi iyaṟki yamippuṭiyavi.
aṭukkuppāṉi, aṭukkuccaṭṭi, aṭukkaḷi (aṭukki vikkappaṭṭa kalaṅkaḷiyuṭiya aṭṭil allatu camiyalaṟi  ākkuppuri eṉpatum atu) aṭukkiyal, aṭukkut toṭar, aṭukkumoḻi eṉpaṉa moḻi vaḻippaṭṭavi. aṭukkiyal arākam eṉappaṭum. ivi ceyaṟkivaḻi yamippuṭiyavi.
aṭukku māṭi nakarkaḷiṉ eḻuccit tōṟṟam aṭukkātīrkaḷ eṉṟāl veṟṟup pukaḻcci vēṇṭā eṉpataṉ kuṟippu.
urupu toṭarn taṭukkiya vēṟṟumik kiḷavi	-tol. 588
aṭukkiya kōṭi	-tiruk.954
aṭukku:2
aṭukku = aintu.
aṭukkup pāṟi aṭukkuppāṉi, aṭukku moḻi, aṭukku malliki eṉpavaṟṟiluḷḷa aṭukku eṉpatu aṭukkutalik kuṟittatu. eṇṇik kuṟittatu illi āṉāl, vāḻi yiliyi oṉṟaṉuḷ oṉṟāka aṭukki ōraṭukku, īraṭukku eṉa ili vaṇikam ceyvar . ivvaṭukku oṉṟil aintu ilikaḷ eṇṇi vikkappaṭum.
aṭukkuc caṭṭi eṉpatu oṉṟaṉuḷ oṉṟu aṭaṅkum. aintu caṭṭikaḷi uṭiyatām. ātalāl aṭukku eṉpatu aintu eṉṉum eṇṇik kuṟittatu. ki, pūṭṭu eṉpavi kāṇka.
aṭukkuḷ 
aṭukku + uḷ= aṭukkuḷ.
camiyalaṟiyi aṭukkuḷ eṉpatu uṇṭu. kalaṅkaḷ aṭukkaṭukkāka vikkappaṭuvatum, vīṭṭiṉ uḷpakutiyāka iruppatum karutiya peyar aṭukkuḷ ākum. itu pārppaṉar vaḻakku.
aṭucil 
aṭucil = cōṟu. aṭucil > aṭicil.
aṭucil ākiya āvuti	-patiṟ.21
aṭiciṟ kiṉiyāḷi 	-taṉip.
x.neh.: 
	oṭuṅki >xo§».
			* aṭicil kāṇka.
aṭutal 
aṭutal = aḻittal; camittal ;  pōriṭal.
aṭappaṭuvatu aṭicil; uṇavu. ataṉi ākkum maṉippakuti aṭṭil, camiyal kūṭam. aṭuttum veṭṭiyum eriyūṭṭiyum ceyyum aṭutal pōl pōrkkaḷattil pakivari aḻittal, aṭutal eṉavum aṭupōr eṉavum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. oṉṟu peṟṟiruntu allatu koṇṭirunta iyalpi allatu niliyi māṟṟiyamittal aṭutalām.
tī aṭuttalāl vētal porital avital mutaliyavum, nīr aṭuttalāl naṉital iḷakal vaḻital mutaliyavum, kāṟṟu aṭuttalāl ulartal vaṟṟal kāytal mutaliyavum, karuvi aṭuttalāl veṭṭal kuttal citittal mutaliyavum ēṟpaṭutal kaṇkūṭu. ākkam kēṭu ākiya iraṇṭum ulakattu iyaṟki ātaliṉ aṭutal payaṉ avviraṇṭumāyk kalantē uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. aḻitalāl payaṉum aḻitalāl kēṭum uṇṭātal veḷippaṭi.
e-ṭu: 
cōṟu paṭukkum tī	-puṟam.20.
ūr cuṭu viḷakku	-maturik.692.
aṭuttum toṭuttum 
aṭuttal 	= 	iṭiveḷipaṭutal.
toṭuttal 	= 	iṭiveḷipaṭāmi.
aṇṭi vīṭu aṭutta vīṭu eṉpatiyum, aṇṭiyar aṭuttavar eṉpatiyum koṇṭu aṭuttal poruḷi aṟika.
toṭar, toṭarcci toṭarvu, toṭali, toṭar, vaṇṭi, toṭar kati, toṭarpoḻivu ivaṟṟāl toṭuttal poruḷi aṟika.
aṭuttu muyaṉṟālum ākunāḷ aṉṟi
eṭutta karumaṅkaḷ ākā - toṭutta 
uruvattāl nīṇṭa uyarmaraṅkaḷ ellām
paruvattāl aṉṟip paḻā	-mūturi 5
aṭuppil kāḷāṉ pūttal 
aṭuppil kāḷāṉ pūttal = camikkavum iyalā vaṟumi.
āmpi, kāḷāmpi, kāḷāṉ eṉpaṉa oru poruḷaṉa. kāḷāṉ kuppiyil peritum uṇṭākum. ātalāl, kuppik kāḷāṉ eṉavum paṭum. nalla maṇ patattil maḻik kālattil tōṉṟak kūṭiya kāḷāṉ camiyal ceyyum aṭuppil muḷittateṉṟāl eṉṉa kāraṇam? pala nāḷkaḷ aṭuppil neruppu mūṭṭavilli. neruppu erinta cāmpali aḷḷavilli. kūri mukaṭu citintiruppatāl maḻinīr vaḻintirukkiṟatu. ākavē, aṅkuk kāḷāṉ tōṉṟiyirukkiṟatu eṉa vaṟumiyiṉ uccattik kāṭṭuvatām. aṭuppil pūṉi kiṇṭutal eṉpatuvum itu.
āṭunaṉi maṟanta kōṭuyar aṭuppiṉ
āmpi pūppa	-puṟam. 164
aṭuppil pūṉi kiṇṭutal 
aṭuppil pūṉi kiṇṭutal = camikkavum iyalā vaṟumi.
pūṉi aḻaku uyiriyāka mēli nāṭṭil vaḷarppatu uṇṭāyiṉum. appaḻakkatti mēṟkoḷḷum nam nāṭṭārum ataṟkāka vaḷarppatu uṇṭāyiṉum, eli piṭippataṟkāka vaḷarppatē perumpāṉmiyām. eli cuvarit tuḷittuk kuṭiyirukkum. vīṭṭut taḷattiyum tōṇṭi kuṭiyirukkumā? palanāḷ aṭuppu mūṭṭāmiyāl atiyum maṟṟa maṇpakuti pōlavē koṇṭu eli vaḷi tōṇṭak kuṭiyirukkiṉṟatām. ataṉip piṭippataṟkākap pūṉi aṭuppik kiṇṭukiṟatām. vaṟumikkoṭumiyic colluvatu itu.
valci iṉmiyiṉ vayiṉvayiṉ māṟi
illeli maṭinta	-puṟam. 211
aṭuppu 
aṭuttu aṭuttu amintatu aṭuppu eṉappaṭṭatu. aṭutal, camittal, camiyal ceyvataṟku aṭuttaṭuttu amikkappaṭuvatu tirukkōyil tirumaṭam maṭippaḷḷikaḷil kāṇalām.
ūrppotu viḻākkaḷ eṭukkappaṭṭup poṅka liṭuṅkāl aimpatu aṟupatu aṭuppukaḷ variciyāka amikkap paṭuvati iṉṟum ciṟṟūrkaḷil kāṇalām.
iṉṟum vīṭukaḷil orē poḻutil palavum ākka vāyppāka vīṭṭu aṭuppukaḷum iraṇṭu mūṉṟu iṇittum, aṭuttum amittal vaḻakkē. āviyaṭuppu vantu viṭṭa nāḷilum iraṇṭu mūṉṟu kalaṅkaḷ vittu ākka aṭuppukaḷ amital nām  kāṇpatē.
aṭuttu amintatāl aṭuppu eṉap peyar koṇṭu atil ākkappaṭum cōṟu kaṟi mutaliyavi aṭutal eṉa āyavi aṟika.
aṭuppum tuṭuppum 
aṭuppu	=	aṭuppu vēli.
tuṭuppu 	= 	aṭuppu vēliyuṭaṉ ceyyum tuṭuppu vēli, kaḷi allatu kūḻ kiṇṭum vēli.
aṭuppil irukkum, uliyiṉ kotinili aṟintu, atil māvip pōṭutalum, pōṭappaṭṭa māvu kaṭṭipaṭṭup pōkāvaṇṇam kiṇṭutalum tīyiṉ aḷaviṉit tēvikkut takak kūṭṭik kuṟittuk koḷḷutalumākiya palatiṟattoru paṇikkiṭiyē, appaṇi ceyvāri evarēṉum kūppiṭṭāl aṭuppum tuṭuppumāka irukkiṟēṉ eṉpār. uṭaṉē vara muṭiyātu eṉpatu maṟumoḻiyām. itaṉi viṭi vaki eṭṭaṉuḷ uṟṟaturittaloṭu cārttalām. allatu piṟitu moḻital viṭiyeṉa oṉpatāy ākkalām.
aṭumpu 
neytal nilakkoṭi vakiyuḷ oṉṟu aṭumpu; aṭampu eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. aṭuttal, neruṅkutal; aṭartal, ceṟital.
aṭu >ml®  > aṭartti.
maram ceṭi koṭi eṉpavaṟṟiṉ pū, kāy, kaṉi mutaliya vaṟṟilum iliyē neruṅkiyum aṭarntum iruppatāl ataṉi aṭi eṉṟaṉar. appotu vakiyām aṭiyiṉum miku ceṟivuṭiya ilikaḷi uṭimiyāl aṭumpu eṉavum aṭampu eṉavum ikkoṭi vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu.
pommal aṭumpu	- naṟ. 272
pommal  > potumpal = ceṟital. potumpu = ceṟinta cōli. potumpil kiḻār eṉpār caṅkappulavar. avar ūr maturi cārnta potumpu.
aṭumpamal neṭuṅkoṭi	eṉpatu aiṅ. taṉip. 6
amalutal	=	neruṅkutal
aṭumpaviḻ aṇimalar citii, taṭantāḷ nāri irukkum ekkareṉpatāl (kuṟun. 349) ataṉ malariṉ parumi pulaṉām.
aṭi 
aṭi: 1
aṭi eṉṉum collukkup pala poruḷkaḷ uṇṭu. aṭi eṉpataṉ vaḻiyāka vanta coṟkaḷum palavām.
aṭi eṉpataṟku ili eṉpatu oru poruḷ. aḵtu eppaṭi vantatu? oru marattil allatu ceṭi koṭikaḷil mikutiyum nirampi allatu aṭarntu iruppatu etu? iliyē. ataṉāl aṭuttaṭuttu neruṅki irukkum ili allatu aṭicalāka mūṭiyirukkum ili aṭi eṉappaṭṭatu. aṭaku eṉiṉum iliyēyām. aṭakeṉṟu colli amutatti iṭṭār eṉpatu auviyār taṉippāṭal.
aṭi: 2
aṭiyuḷ ōr ilikkut taṉic ciṟappuṇṭu. maṅkalat taṉmiyum ataṟkut tarappaṭuvatuṇṭu. atu veḷḷaṭi eṉappaṭum veṟṟili.
iḷantaḷir, veḷuppu niṟamāka irutatalāl veḷḷaṭi eṉappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. ataṟku veṟṟilip peyar eppaṭi vantatu? pūvāta kāyāta ili, veṟṟu ili tāṉē! iṉi, veṟumaṉē - aviyāmal, kāyap pōṭāmal, kaṭital illāmal - virumpit tiṉṉum iliyāka iruppatālum veṟṟili āyiṟṟu. veṟumi ili, veṟṟili.
aṭikāttal 
aṭikāttal 	= veḷippōkātu vīṭṭuḷ iruttal.
kōḻi muṭṭiyiṭṭu irupattoru nāḷ aṭi kiṭakkum. kuñcu porittu veḷippaṭum vari aṭi kākkum kōḻi. tīṉi nīr ākiyavaṟṟiyum karutuvatu illi. aṭiyi viṭṭu veḷippaṭavum eḷitil virumpuvatu illi. aṭi kiṭakkat taṭiyoṉṟu varuvatāyiṉ ataṉi etirttup pōriṭavum tuṇiyum. avvaṭi kākkum vaḻakkattiliruntu, kiṭanta kiṭappi viṭṭō, vīṭṭi viṭṭō veḷippaṭāmal iruttal eṉṉum poruḷ ataṟkup piṟantatu. vīṭṭi viṭṭu veḷippōkāta piḷḷikaḷi ēṉ aṭi kiṭakkiṟāy? veḷiyē pōy vāyēṉeṉpatu vaḻakkāyiṟṟu.
aṭikkattu 
muṭṭiyiṭutaṟkuk kōḻi kattutal (kēṟutal) aṭikkattu eṉappaṭum. akkōḻi muṭṭiyiṭṭu aṭi kākkap paruvam vantati ataṉ moḻiyāl kūṟutalē kēṟutal, ataṉi uṇarntu aṭi kūṭṭi vikkat tavaṟiṉāl, nam vīṭṭilē tiṉṟum kuṭittum, aṭutta vīṭṭilē muṭṭiyiṭṭu viṭṭu vantu viṭum.
aṭi eṉpatu eṉṉa? kōḻi kuñcu porippataṟkāka vikkappaṭum maṇ ōṭu. atil umiyip parappi vittuviṭṭāl atilēyē muṭṭiyiṭum; aṅkēyē paṭuttuk koḷḷum. tīṉi taṇṇīr karutāmal pala nāḷkaḷ aṭintē kiṭappataṟkuriya iruppi aṭieṉpatu caritāṉē! kāṭṭup paṟavikaḷ tāmē īṉil iḻittuk koṇṭāl, vīṭṭup paṟaviyām kōḻikku nāmē aṭi vikkiṟōm. tāymik kōyil avvaṭi aṉṟō!
aṭikkalam 
ūr viṭṭu ūr pōvār. vili matippu mikka tam poruḷkaḷit takkāriṭam tantu pātukākkac colvatu paḻavaḻakku, avvaḻakkiliruntu vantatē aṭikkalam eṉpatu. kalam, aṇikalam, uṇkalam, paṭikkalam ākiya iṉṉavi.
ivvaḻakkē nampikki yuṭiyavarkaḷiṭam maṉivi makkaḷ mutaliyavarkaḷi aṭikkalamāka vikkum nilimikku vaḷarntatu. ippoḻutu aṭikkalam iṉṉum virintu viṭṭatu. nāṭṭukku nāṭē aṭikkalamākap pōyk koṇṭuḷḷati varalāṟu kāṭṭik koṇṭuḷḷatu.
aṭikkalam makkaḷ peyarum, teyvap peyarum, ūrp peyarumāka viḷaṅkutal veḷippaṭi.
aṭikkalāṅ kuruvi 
kuruvikaḷuḷ oṉṟu aṭikkalāṅ kuruvi. atu, kuṭici, kūri, mukaṭu, palakaṇi ākiyavaṟṟil taṅki iruppatu. atu aṭikkalamāka vantu taṅkiyiruppatāl ataṟkuk kēṭu cūḻak kuṭicivāṇar eṇṇavum ceyyār. aṭikkala eṇṇap pēṟu atu.
aṭikkāy 
veṟṟili pōṭupavar aṭiyākiya veṟṟiliyi maṭṭumā mellukiṉṟaṉar? ataṉōṭu pākkum cērkkap paṭukiṉṟatu. ap pākkukkup peyar aṭikkāy eṉpatu. aṭikāy eṉiṉ aṭiyum ataṉōṭu cērkkum kāyum eṉap poruḷām. aṭikkāy eṉiṉ pākku eṉṟām.
aṭical 
nila pulaṅkaḷil payir nirampiyiruntāl payir aṭica lākak kiṭakkiṟatu eṉpatum, kaḷi nirampiyiruntāl kaḷi aṭicalākak kiṭakkiṟatu eṉpatum uḻavaṭic coṟkaḷ. uḻavut toḻili aṭuttut tōṉṟiya col uḻavaṭic coltāṉē!
aṭittal 
 uṭippu, paḷḷam ākiyavaṟṟi muṟiyē aṭippatum mūṭuvatum pōl vayiṟu muṭṭa uṭkoḷal aṭittalām. katavaṭittal pōlavum, vaḻiyaṭittal pōlavum (tiruk. 38  puṟam. 151) uṇavu pukuvāy muṭṭa uṇpatu eṉiṉum oppatē.
puṭṭaval paṭṭāṇi poritēṅ kuḻalappam.
maṭṭaviḻum tōci vaṭiyuṭaṉē - caṭṭamuṭaṉ
ōyāmal cōṟukaṟi yuṇṭiyuṇṭi yāy aṭikkum
vāyā namaccivā yā	- taṉip. 4  823
aṭittuk koḷḷal 
aṭittuk koḷḷal = irumalamum veḷippaṭāmi.
veḷippaṭāmal mūṭi vippati aṭittal eṉpatu vaḻakku. katavaṭippu, ciṟiyaṭippu, taṭṭiyaṭippu, peṭṭiyaṭippu eṉpavaṟṟik karutuka.
oruvar aṭikkāmal tāṉē aṭittuk koḷḷum ciṟunīrkkaṭṭu, malakkaṭṭu eṉpaviyum, aṭittuk koḷḷalākak kūṟappaṭum. muṉṉum piṉṉum aṭittuk koṇṭatu eṉpatum vaḻakkē. kurutiyōṭṭat taṭippāṭu, mūccōṭṭat taṭippāṭu eṉpaṉavum aṭittuk koḷḷal eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. mārpaṭippu nōy mikap peruki varuvatu kaṇkūṭu.
aṭittōyi (aṭittōci)  
aṭiyiṉ cuviyi aṟiyār evar? aṭit tōciyi virumpār evar? ataṟku aṭi eṉa eppaṭip peyar vantatu?
paruppu vaki, tēṅkāy, miḷakāy, kaṟivēppili, iñci mutaliya palavum cērttu ākkappaṭuvatu aṭi. aḵtu oru kālattil perumpālum. kīri vakikaḷi āṭṭip paruppu mutalivaṟṟuṭaṉ kalantu ākkiyamiyāl vanta peyar eṉpati veḷiyiṭukiṟatu. pala poruḷkaḷ ceṟintu amintatu. aṉṟiyum attōyi māttōyiyilum kaṉamāṉatumkūṭa.
iliyaṭiyāka irunta aṭi muṭṭi yaṭiyākavum mālik kaṭikaḷil cāli vaḻiyellām iṭam peṟa lāyiṟṟē. avvaṭi tēnīrk kaṭikaḷilellām kūṭa iṭam piṭittuk koṇṭatē. atilum maṟṟic cuvip poruḷkaḷ pala iṭampiṭittuk koṇṭaṉa.
* tōyi kāṇka.
aṭippāṉ 
āṭu māṭukaḷukku aṭippāṉ, nōy uṇmiyi evar aṟiyār? kaṭiyaṭippukaḷ, katavaṭippukaḷ ākiyavi ceytittāḷ carakkāyiṉavē! aṭiyā neṭuṅ katavu (kampar taṉip) tamiḻaṉ paṇpāṭṭuc cāṉṟu eṉpatu iṉṟu naṭimuṟiyāmā? kaṭittiṟappup pāṭāmalē katavu tiṟantu, tōlirukkaccuḷi viḻuṅkiyatu pōla nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷ tamiḻ maṇṇilum kūṭat tavaḻalāyiṉavē
aṭippu  
uṭippu eṭuttāl aṭuttu aṭippu uṇṭaṉṟō! aṭi eṉpatu ēvalāyiṟṟē! maṇ, maṇal, kal, ili taḻi mutaliyavaṟṟic ceṟittu vippatu aṭippu. aṇi eṉpatu aṭippuc ceykitāṉē! aṇṇutal, neruṅkutal, aṭuttal, aṇṇutal aṇi, aṭuttal aṭi; kuḻanti puraṇṭu viṭāmal iruppataṟkut tāy eṉṉa ceykiṟār? aṇiyaṭi tuṇiyāl amikkiṟār.
uṭippu aṭippu maṭṭumtāṉā aṭippu! ip poḻutu aṭippup peruki varutal kaṇkūṭu. māraṭippu, muṉṉum piṉṉum aṭippu.ippaṭi ettaṉi aṭippukaḷ?
aṭippi 
muṟkālattil peruñcelvarkaḷ, ciṟṟaracarkaḷ āki yōrkku veṟṟili maṭittut taruvataṟku ēvalāḷarkaḷ vittiruntaṉar. avarkaḷukku aṭippikkārar eṉpatu peyar. aṭippi veṟṟilippi.
maṭit toḻil (camiyal toḻil) vallārkaḷip paṇiyāḷarkaḷākac celvarkaḷ amarttiyiruntatu pōlavē, aṭippikkārarkaḷiyum amarttiyiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ, niṉivūṭṭat takka vakiyil cuviyum maṇamum mikka veṟṟilic curuḷkaḷ amittut tantaṉar; iṭittup patappaṭuttiyum tantaṉar.
celvac ceḻippiṉ aṭiyāḷamākavum makiḻvuk kuṟiyākavum maṅkalak kuṟiyākavum veṟṟili āyatāl ataṉi vikkum peṭṭi veḷḷiyāṉum poṉṉālum vēlippāṭu mikkatākac ceyalpaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. ataṟku, veṟṟilic celvam cellap peṭṭi (celvap peṭṭi) eṉṉum vaḻakkam uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
tamaṉiya aṭippi eṉpataṟkup poṉṉāṟ ceyta veṟṟilip peṭṭi eṉṟār arumpata urikārar (cilap. 14 128) cīrkoḷac ceyta cempoṉ aṭippieṉṟum, erimaṇi aṭippieṉṟum cintāmaṇi kūṟiṟṟu. (1303, 2140) aracar cuṟṟaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāka, aṭippic cuṟṟattic cuṭṭiṟṟu peruṅkati (1 38 161)
aṭimāṉam 
oruvariṭam oru poruḷi oppaṭittu ataṟku īṭāka matikkattakka poruḷip peṟṟuk koṇṭu piṉṉart tām vāṅkiya poruḷi vaṭṭiyoṭu tantu tām aṭivu vitta poruḷi mīṭṭuk koḷḷal aṭimāṉamām.
māṉam = aḷavu. aṭimāṉam = oppaṭitta poruḷ aḷavu. aṭimāṉa muriyum eḻutap peṟaluṇṭu. māṉam, col īṟu, varumāṉam peṟumāṉam pōlvatu eṉavum koḷḷalām. aṭimāṉam makkaḷ vaḻakkil aṭamāṉam eṉappaṭukiṟatu.
aṭimuṇṭu 
oru vaṇṭiyi aciyāmal niṟutta vēṇṭum. ataṟku aṭi vippar. cakkarattiṉ muṉṉum piṉṉum nerukkamāka aṭuttuk koṭuppatē aṭi. atu muṉṉaṭi piṉṉaṭi eṉṟu iraṇṭākac collavum paṭum. aṭimuṇṭu, aṭikkal eṉpavi avi.
aṭimoḻi 
tāmariyi veṇṭāmari, centāmari eṉṟu aṭi tantu coṉṉāl, ataṉ vaki viḷakkamākiṉṟatē! ceṅkāl nāri naṉicuvark kūri mūvariyaṇil 
kōṭuvāḻ kuraṅku eṉṟu aṭimoḻikaḷ tantāl poruḷ viḷakkamum naṭi nayamum amital viḷaṅkum. ivi peyaraṭi, viṉiyaṭi, eṉpaṉavām.
e-ṭu: 
veṇṭāmari	-  peyaraṭi  veṇmi.
nimirntu nil	-	viṉiyaṭi; nimirntu.
aṭiyal 
aṭi+al = aṭiyal.
ūri aṭital, aluvalakatti aṭital eṉa aṭital, cērtal poruḷil varum aṭiyal kurukē aṭiyaleṅkāṉal eṉpatu cilampu. aṭiyal, aṭiyātē eṉṉum poruḷatu.
aṭiyār 
aṭi + ār = aṭiyār
oruvari aṭintu neruṅki iruppavar naṭpar. avvāṟu aṭiyār naṭparallar. avar aṭiyār, aṭiyalar. iccoṟkaḷukkup pakivar eṉpatu poruḷ.
aṭiyāḷam 
aṭiyāḷam kāṇavē oru tuṟiyeṉṉa, vaki vakit tuṟikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. tali ippaṭi; mukam ippaṭi; maccam taḻumpu ippaṭi ippaṭi amippu, cūḻal ippaṭi ippaṭi;  oruvari allatu oṉṟi, aṭintuḷḷa kuṟikaḷik kaṇṭu teḷivatē aṭiyāḷam ākum.
oruvar tam kaṇṇilum ceviyilum karuttilum aṭintuḷḷa tōṟṟamum amivum māṟṟamillāmi aṭiyāḷamām. āḷiṉ tōṟṟamē mutaṟkaṇ patintu viṭṭamiyāl aṭiyāḷam eṉappaṭṭu, piṉṉē āṭu māṭu tōṭṭam turavu aṇimaṇi mutaliyaṉavum aṭiyāḷam āyatu.
aṭiyāḷam ottiruttalāl paḻiyōriṭam pāvamōriṭam  eṉa māṟi viṭuvatum putumiyilli. kuṟṟa vaḻakkil aṭiyāḷam paṭuttum pāṭu taṉit toṉmamām (purāṇamām).
eṉṉa! ippaṭi iḷittu viṭṭīrkaḷ? aṭiyāḷamē teriyavilli
āḷvanta aṭiyāḷamē pulappaṭavilli!
eṉpaṉavellām vaḻakkil uḷḷavi.
aṭivukkaṭi 
innāḷil pala iṭaṅkaḷil aṭakuk kaṭi eṉap palakikaḷ toṅkak kāṇukiṟōmē? avi kīrik kaṭikaḷā? aṭaku eṉpatu kīri allavā!
aṭakeṉṟu colli amutatti iṭṭār	- auvi taṉip.
nam poruḷ oṉṟi oppaṭittu, oppaṭikkappaṭṭa variṭamiruntu namakku vēṇṭum poruḷi vāṅkik koḷvatu aṭivu ākum. ataṉic ceyyum kaṭikaḷ aṭivuk kaṭikaḷ!
*aṭimāṉam kāṇka.
aṭṭakam (eṭṭakam) 
orē vaki yāppil pāṭalkaḷ eṭṭup pāṭuvatu aṭṭakam ākum. vaṇṇac carapam taṇṭapāṇiyaṭikaḷ pāṭiyatu mantira aṭṭakam. eṭṭeḻuttip paṟṟik kūṟum eṭṭup pāṭalkaḷi uṭiyatu. itu tiruvaraṅkat tiruvāyirattil uḷḷatu.
aṭṭakācam 
orē aṭṭakācamāka irukkiṟatu eṉṟu pārāṭṭu kiṟōm. avar arumiyāka uṭuttum, arumiyāka oppaṉi ceytum polivōṭu kāṭci vaḻaṅkukiṟār; avarip pārttu, orē aṭṭakācam pōṅkaḷeṉkiṟōm. cilar vīṭu tōṭṭac cūḻal ākiyavaṟṟip pārtta aḷavil aṭṭakācamāṉa vīṭu eṉkiṟōm. inta aṭṭakācam eṉpataṉ poruḷeṉṉa?
cilar cirikkum pōtu peruñcirippuc cirittaluṇṭu. kuṟunaki, aḷavē naki, perunaki eṉa munnakip paṭuttik kūṟuvar paḻiya uriyāciriyarkaḷ, kuṟukac cirittal, aḷavē cirittal, perukac cirittal eṉpavi avi. naki eṉpataṟkē oḷipporuḷ uṇṭu. pala malikaḷiyum pārttut taṉ eṭuppālum vaḷattālum, vaṉappālum oḷiyālum nakikkiṉṟatām paṉimali ataṉāl,
paṉṉakamum nakuveḷḷip paṉivari
eṉṟār kalviyil periyavar. (pāla. 324) poṉṉakikkum puṉṉakikkum poruntiya oṟṟumi iruppati namnāṭu ulakukkup paṟiyaṟintu koṇṭutāṉē uḷḷatu.
aṭṭakācam eṉpataṟkup perunaki eṉap poruḷ tarukiṉṟaṉa akara mutalikaḷ. polivu, perunaki ākiya ivaṟṟi aṭṭakācam eṉṉum col eppaṭit tarukiṟatu?
aṭṭālum pāl cuviyil kuṉṟātu, cuṭṭālum caṅku oḷiyil kuṉṟātueṉpavi auviyār uri. aṭutalum cuṭutalum oru viṉi iru coṟkaḷē! cuṇṭak kāyccappaṭṭa pālil cuviyēṟavē ceyyum! caṅku cuṭṭāl veṇmi mikuvatu, cippic cuṇṇāmpip pārttālē pulappaṭum. veḷḷiyaṭippukkeṉa ataṉit tēṭip pōy vāṅkuvatu ataṉ iṇiyilā veṇmikkētāṉ! cuṭṭa cippikkuc - caṅkukku - evvaḷavu veṇmi! cuṭṭatu curukkum, kariyākum. itu veḷukkiṟatu. mēlum veḷukkiṟatu.
ippaṭiyē cuṭṭāl oḷi mikuvatum oṉṟuṇmi aṉivarum aṟintatē. atu taṅkam. cuṭaccuṭa ataṟku oḷimikum ataṉāl tiruvaḷḷuvar.
cuṭaccuṭarum poṉpōl oḷiviṭum tuṉpam
cuṭaccuṭa nōṟkiṟ pavarkku
eṉṟār (267). poṉ cuṭaccuṭa oḷiviṭum. māntar, tuṉpam cuṭaccuṭa oḷiviṭuvar eṉpatām.
cuṭṭu uruki niṟkum poṉṉip pārttāl ataṉ polivu pulappaṭum. poṉṉil ciṟanta poṉ āṇip poṉ. kalappaṟṟa tūya poṉ eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟāka uriyāṇiyiṭṭa poṉṉē āṇippoṉ. ōṭaviṭṭa poṉ eṉpatum vaḻakku ōṭaviṭutal eṉpatu. aḵtoṉṟē iṟutiyil taṅkum eñciyavi āviyāy aḻintu pōm; ātalāl ataṉic taṅkam eṉṟaṉar eṉpar.
mālip poḻutil katirōṉ kāṭciyik kāṇukiṉṟār pāvēntar.
taṅkatti urukkiviṭṭa vāṉōṭi taṉṉilē ōr ceṅkatir māṇikkattuc ceḻum paḻam muḻukum māli eṉkiṟār.

eṉṟu vaṇṇippatu karutat takkatu.
poṉ, cuṭar ivaṟṟip paṟṟi ivaṇ virippāṉēṉ? poṉṉukku oru peyar kācu. kācumāli eṉpatu poṉṉāl ceyta māliyē; kācuk kaṭi eṉpatu poṟkaṭiyē; allatu taṅkak kaṭiyē!
kiḷi vēppam paḻattic civanta alakāl paṟṟukiṟatu. ataṉi, ōraḻaki poṟkācil nūl nuḻippataṟkākat taṉ civanta viralkaḷiṉ iṭiyē paṟṟiyiruppataṟku uvami kāṭṭukiṟār oru pulavar (kuṟun. 67) taṅkattāl ceyta paṇamē kācu eṉappaṭṭu, ataṉ piṉṉarc cempu mutaliyavaṟṟāl ceytataṟkum āyiṟṟu eṉpatu nāṇaya varalāṟṟuc ceyti.
kācu poṉ, polam eṉavum vaḻaṅkutal, poṉṉil iruntu poṟpu aḻaku eṉpavi yuṇṭām. kācu ākiya poṉṉiṉ polivu ulakaṟinta ceyti. appoṉ takattaka eṉap paḷicciṭutal pulavarkaḷik kavarntatu pōlavē potu makkaḷiyum kavarntamiyāl ēṟpaṭṭatē aṭṭakācam!
aṭṭa kācu cuṭupoṉ - cuṭarppoṉ - takattaka eṉa oḷiviṭum aḻaku aṭṭakācam; kācu +am =kācam itaṉoṭu ōr oṭṭic cērttup pirakācam (prakācam) eṉṟār piṟar.
tēm tēem eṉpavi tēyamāki, tēcamāki viḷaṅkutal terintataṉṟō?
kāyam eṉṉum tol paḻantamiḻc col ā eṉpati oṭṭi ākāyamāy, vēṟṟuc collākic centamiḻc colli vīḻtti viṭavilliyā?
aṭṭakācam, cuṭṭapoṉ! cuṭaccuṭarum cuṭarppoṉ! vaḷḷuvar arikkuṟaḷ, potumakkaḷ pārviyil aṭṭa kācamāka irukkiṟatu eṉpatu itaṉāl viḷaṅkum.
aṭṭakkari 
aṭṭakkari aṭṭak kaṟuppu eṉa mikak kaṟuppāka irukkum oṉṟic cuṭṭutaṟku uvamip paṭuttik kūṟukiṟōm. avaṉā? m£l¡ fÇ!”, atuvā? aṭṭak kaṟuppu!ippaṭic colli viṭutal peruvaḻakku.
ivviru vaṭivaṅkaḷiyum akaravari nūlkaḷ tantu, mikka kaṟuppueṉapporuḷ tarukiṉṟaṉa.
aṭṭakkari, aṭṭak kaṟuppu = mikka kaṟuppu.
ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻaka akarāti.
aṭṭakkari ATTA-K-KARI, N. CT. ATTA+JET BLACK. DENCE BLACKNESS. mikka kaṟuppu CALLOG.
aṭṭakkaṟuppu ATTA-K- KARUP. PU.N. CT. IDT JET BLACK. mikak kaṟuppu.
āṉanta vikaṭaṉ akarāti. ma.ta.ca.akarātic ceytiyi appaṭiyē amittuk koṇṭatu.
pala akara mutalikaḷil iccoṟkaḷ iṭam peṟavilli. centamiḻc coṟpiṟappiyal pērakara mutaliyil iccoṟkaḷ iṭam peṟṟatuṭaṉ viḷakkamum peṟṟuḷa.
aṭṭakkari  ATT-A-L-KARI. bg.  (N) mikak karuppu mikak karuppāṉatu; ma. aṭṭakkari JET BLACK, THAT WHICH IS JET BLACK.
(orukā. aṇṭam (aṇṭaṅkākki) pōṉṟa eṉṟirukkalām.  o.nō:  naṇṭuvāyk kāli naṭṭuvāykkāli)
aṭṭak karuppu ATTA-K-KARUPPU. bg. (N) mikak karuppu mikka karuppu JET BLACK.”
aṭṭam eṉṉum collukkup poruḷ teḷivāki viṭṭāl kari, kaṟuppuk kavaliyilli. aṭṭam eṉpataṟkuk kūṟiya mika, mikka eṉṟa poruḷkaḷ cariyāṉaviyē. āyiṉum eppaṭi apporuḷ tantatu eṉṟa kuṟippu illi.
aṇṭaṅkākkiyi aṭṭak kākki eṉṟālum atē cikkaltāṉ. vēṟṟu moḻi oppum vaḻakkoppē yaṉṟip poruḷ viḷakka oppu illiyām.
aṭṭam eṉṟoru poruḷ uṇṭu. avvāṟē kiṭṭam  eṉṟoru poruḷum uṇṭu. avaṟṟi aṟital iv viḷakkattiṟku oḷitarum.
oḷiyit tēṭittāṉē pōka vēṇṭum; ulik kaḷattil kariyi uliyil pōṭṭu vēka vittu avvēkkāṭṭil irumpi vetupputal evarum aṟintatu. erintu iṟukip pōṉa kari iṟuki aṭarppuṟṟu irumpeṉac ceṟintu kaṭṭiyākum. ataṟkuk kiṭṭam eṉpatu peyar. oppumiyāl, eṉṉa itu irumpuk kiṭṭamāka irukkiṟatueṉa uṭipaṭāp poruḷik kūṟuvatu vaḻakku. kariyāl ōṭum toṭar vaṇṭiyiliruntu eriporuḷit taḷḷuṅkāl āṅkum kiṭṭam kāṇalām.
viṟaku ,eru mutaliyavaṟṟāl eriyum aṭuppil vikkap paṭṭa camiyal kalaṅkaḷ, akkarip pukiyāl kaṟuttup pōkum. aṭṭa puki appip paṟṟik koḷḷum. kalattiṉ vaṇṇam, evvaṇṇam āyiṉum karuvaṇṇamākki viṭum. paṟṟip piṭikkum akkari. puṟattē eṉṟāl uḷḷēyum paṟṟip piṭikkum poruḷum uṇṭu. ataṉāl paṟṟut tēyttal eṉpatu oru toḻilāy - oru piḻippāy amintuviṭṭatu. aṭṭatāl aminta karippaṟṟu eṉṉum poruḷkaḷum kiḷarntaṉa.
aṭukalattu ēṟpaṭum kariyē aṭṭakkari, akkaṟuppē aṭṭakkaṟuppu eṉappaṭalāyiṟṟu. cōṟṟup pāṉi pōla niṟam  kaṟic caṭṭi pōla niṟameṉpavi vaḻaṅku moḻikaḷ.
orunāy, paṉṟi vēṭṭikkup pōṉatām; paṉṟi oṉṟik kaṇṭu nāy neruṅkiyatām; tuṇivu mikka paṉṟi nāyip puraṭṭip puraṭṭip paṭāppāṭu paṭutti viṭṭatām! ataṉāl vēṭṭikkaḷattil iruntu vīṭṭukku vanta nāy, kaḻuvuvataṟkāka vikkappaṭṭirunta cōṟṟup pāṉiyik kaṇṭatum taṉṉik kāṭṭil veruṭṭiya paṉṟiyē vīṭṭil niṟpatāka eṇṇi āṅkiruntu ōṭṭam piṭittatām. ikkatiyi uṭkoṇṭu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu oru paḻamoḻi.
paṉṟi vēṭṭiyil payantu vanta nāy, cōṟṟup pāṉiyik kaṇṭu ōṭṭameṭuttatāmeṉpatu atu. ippaḻamoḻiyum aṭṭattic cuṭṭutal aṟika.
aṭṭam + kari	   =   aṭṭakkari
aṭṭam + kaṟuppu  =    aṭṭakkaṟuppu
 iṟukkap paṟṟik koḷḷutal aṭṭam eṉappaṭṭa piṉṉē, annili aṭṭu eṉappaṭalāyiṟṟu. aṭṭup piṭittavaṉ taliyellām aṭṭuppiṭittuk kiṭattal aḻukkup piṭittalāl, taliyil cikkup piṭittal, caṭiyātal iviyellām paṟṟālum pācattālum viḷivaṉa allavā!
aṭṭaṇikkāl 
aṭu  > aṭṭu  + aṇi + kāl = aṭṭaṇikkāl
aṭutal = kuṟukkiṭutal, taṭuttal. ōr irukkiyilō tiṇṇiyilō amarntu orukāli nilattil ūṉṟi, maṟṟoru kāli ūṉṟiya kāl mēl kuṟukkākap pōṭṭu amartal aṭṭaṇikkāl ākum.
eṉṉa, aṭṭaṇikkāl pōṭṭu amarntuviṭṭīrkaḷ; vēlikkup pōkavilliyā? eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
aṭṭamaṇiyam 
vaṭṭam cuṟṟi vaḻiyē pō eṉṟāl aṭṭattil pāyntu viṭṭāṉ eṉpatu vaḻakkuc col. aṭṭam eṉpatu kuṟukkē eṉṉum poruḷatu.
naṭṭaṇikkāl eṉpatu nilattil ūṉṟiya kāl; aṭṭaṇikkāl eṉpatu naṭṭaṇikkāl mēl kuṟukkākat tūkkip pōṭṭa kāl, vaṭṭam muḻuccuṟṟu; aṭṭam kuṟukku.
aṭṭamaṇiyam eṉṉum coṟṟoṭaril aṭṭam eṉpataṟkuk kuṟukku eṉṉum poruḷ teḷivākiṟatu. maṇiyam eṉpataṉ poruḷ eṉṉa?
nāṭṭāṭciyiṉ ciṟiya aḷavu, ūrāṭci pallāyiram ūrāṭcikaḷiṉ tokuppē nāṭṭāṭci, ūrāṭci ceypavar ūrmaṇiyam (kirāma maṇiyam) avarē ūriṉ āṭciyāḷar.
āṅkilar āṭciyil intiyap parappukkut tuṇiyaracar (Vice Ray) iruntār. mānila āṭcikku āḷunar (GOVERNOR) iruntār. avvāṟē māvaṭṭa āṭciyil iruntu ūrāṭci vari āṭciyāḷar iruntaṉar. annaṭimuṟi innāḷ vari toṭaral kaṇkūṭu. ūr aluvalar (Village Officer) eṉṉum peyarāl iṉṟu uḷḷaṉar.
ūrāṭci pārttavar,  ūr maṇiyam eṉappaṭṭār. avar vittatu varici; ceytatu caṭṭam; vari vāṅkutalā, taṇṭat tīrvi vitittalā, ūr vaḻakki muṭittalā, piṟappu iṟappuk kuṟippā avvaḷavukkum avarē poṟuppāḷar. mēlāṭciyāḷar evar variṉum ūrmaṇiyattip pārttuttāṉ etuvum ceyvār. maṇiyam coṉṉatutāṉ aracu coṉṉatu. avar coṉṉatē kāvaltuṟi, nītittuṟic cāṉṟukaḷ.
maṇiyam ūril maṇi pōṉṟavar eṉap paṭṭavartām. āyiṉum ōṭṭi uṭical kīṟalmaṇikaḷ illāmal pōvatu illiyē. muṟi ceyya vēṇṭiya avar, muṟi kēṭarāka amivatum tavirkka muṭiyātatu tāṉē! ataṉāl, maṇiyam eṉpataṟkuriya poruḷ atikāram ceytal, muṟi kēṭāka atikāram ceytal eṉpaṉavāy amintaṉa. ataṉāl, inta maṇiyamellām eṉṉiṭam vittuk koḷḷātē uṉṉi yār  maṇiyattiṟku vittatu? eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa vaḻakkukaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. avvakiyil vanta oṉṟē aṭṭamaṇiyam eṉpatu.
oru vēliyi mēṟpārkka or aluvalaṉ uḷḷāṉ. avaṉē keṭupiṭikkāraṉ; īvu irakkamillāmal naṭappavaṉ. avaṉ illāp poḻutil allatu irukkum poḻutil kūṭa, avaṉukkum keṭupiṭi ceypavaṉāka oruvaṉ talippaṭṭāl, avaṉ ceyyum keṭupiṭi ericcalil piṟantatu. aṭṭamaṇiyam. avaṉ ceyyum maṇiyattip poṟuttālum ivaṉ ceyyum aṭṭamaṇiyattip poṟukka muṭiyavillieṉṟu vetumpi yurippatu vaḻakkāyiṟṟu.
aṭṭamaṇiyam eṉpatu kuṟukkē kuṟukkē pukuntu vēṇṭāta vakiyil  ēṉ? veṟukkum vakiyil atikāram ceyvatām. ūriṉ maṇiyeṉappaṭṭa perumi, veṭṭi atikārap poruḷukku iṟaṅkiya iṟaṅkunilip poruḷ itu.
ūrmaṇiyam pārttavar maṇiyakārar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. atu kuṭivaḻip peyarāy - ōr iṉap peyarāy - innāḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. kaṇakku eḻutiya kuṭivaḻiyiṉar karuṇīkar eṉappaṭṭatu pōṉṟatu itu.
ciṟṟūr kōyil maṭam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ mēlāṇmi ceypavar maṇiyam maṇiyakāraṉ eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka akarāti. immaṇiyattiṉ tuṭippu viḷaṅkumāṟu, tuṭi maṇiyakkārar eṉkiṟatu viṟali viṭu tūtu (1055). maṇiyattiṉ aṭāvaṭittaṉam pulappaṭumāṟu turccaṉar tamakkēṟṟa maṇiyam eṉkiṟatu aṟappaḷīcurar catakam (49)
aṭṭa maṇiyam pārāṭṭāl veḷippaṭṭatu aṉṟu. poṟukka māṭṭā ericcalāl veḷippaṭṭatu. patavip peyar paḻip peyarāyataṟku iḵtoru cāṉṟu. 
aṭṭam 
vaṭṭattiṉ kuṟukkē amintatu aṭṭam. aṭṭam cuḻikkāmal eṉpatu ūṭu allatu kuṟukkē pōkāmal eṉpatām viḷiyāṭṭil payaṉpaṭuttum col itu. nelli vaḻakku.
aṭṭaḷi 
aṭu >m£L + aḷi = aṭṭaḷi.
kuṟukkum neṭukkumākap palakiyālō irumput takaṭṭālō taṭuttut taṭṭut taṭṭākap poruḷkaḷi vikkac ceytatu, aṭṭaḷiyām. vīṭṭuppayaṉ, aṅkāṭip payaṉ āyavaṟṟukku iṉṟiyamiyātatu aṭṭaḷi.
aṭṭāli 
matilmēl uṟuppukaḷuḷ oṉṟu aṭṭāli. atil iruntu kākkum vīrar aṭṭālic cēvakar eṉappaṭuvar. aṭṭaḷi eṉpatu cuvaril poruntum tāṅku caṭṭam allatu palaki. koṅku nāṭṭil aṭṭāli eṉpatu paraṇ eṉṉum kāval  amippik kuṟittu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. paraṇi eṉpatum atu.
aṭṭi 
nīṅkaḷ nāḷikku vantu aṭṭiyillāmal vāṅkik koṇṭu pōkalām eṉpatu teṉ tamiḻaka vaḻakkuc col aṭṭi = taṭi . aṭuttu vikkum muṭṭu - muṇṭu - aṭi. atu. nakaraviṭāmal taṭuppatu taṭi illāmal - taṭuppu illāmal. atupōl aṭi illāmal, aṭuttuḷḷa taṭiyillāmal eṉpatu aṭṭi āyiṟṟām. aṭu > aṭṭu > aṭṭi.
aṭṭil 
aṭu > aṭṭu + il = aṭṭil camiyaṟ pakuti aṭutal, camittal.
camiyal ceyya aminta pēriṭam aṭṭiṟ cāli, aṭṭiṟ kūṭam, maṭippaḷḷi eṉap pala peyar ēṟkum. maṭittoḻil, camiyal vēli.
aṭumaṭiyā	-naḷaveṇpā (385).
aṭṭukkuñcu 
poritta kōḻik kuñci aṭṭukkuñcu eṉpatu karuvūr vaṭṭāra vaḻakku. aṭu >m£L = neruṅki.
aṭṭukkuvi 
aṭṭukkuvi: 1
kuvi = kuvikkappaṭṭa kuviyal. aṭṭukkuvi cōṟu = camittu malipōl kuvikkappaṭṭacōṟu.
aṭṭukkuvi cōṟṟup paruppatam	-nālā. 264
aṭṭukkuvi: 2
aṭṭu	 =	 koṉṟu.
aṭṭukkuvi	 = 	koṉṟukuvi ; kaḷappōril talivaṉ ēval.
aṭṭūḻiyam 
aṭṭu + ūḻiyam =aṭṭūḻiyam.
aṭṭūḻiyam eṉpataṟkut tīmpu eṉkiṟatu ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻaka akarāti. maturit tamiḻccaṅka akarātiyum atiyē kūṟukiṟatu. koṭuntīmpu eṉkiṟatu centamiḻc coṟpiṟappup pērakara mutali; avaṉ paṇṇiya aṭṭūḻiyat tiṟku aḷavillieṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭu tantu, maliyāḷam. kaṉṉaṭam ākiya moḻikaḷilum iccollāṭci yuṇmiyiyum colkiṟatu.
aṭṭūḻiyam vanta vaki eṉṉa? aṭṭūḻiyattiṟkut tīmpu eṉa eppaṭip poruḷ vantatu? tīmpu eṉpataṟkut tīmi eṉpatik kāṭṭik, kuṟumpu kēṭu eṉṉum poruḷkaḷit tarukiṟatu ce. pa . ka. akarāti.
aṭṭūḻiyam ceypavaṉ, talivaṉākap - periyavaṉāka- āṇiyiṭat takka takutiyō muṟiyō urimiyō uṭiyavaṉāka irukka māṭṭāṉ. avaṉ ceykiṉṟa vēṇṭāta taliyīṭukaḷ muṟikēṭukaḷ, kuṟukkīṭukaḷ ākiyaviyē aṭṭūḻiyam eṉappaṭukiṉṟatām. itaṉi eṇṇip pārttuk koṇṭē collamippi nōkkutal vēṇṭum.
aṭṭu eṉpatum ūḻiyam eṉpatum ākiya irucol iṇippē aṭṭūḻiyam eṉpatu veḷippaṭi. itil aṭṭu eṉpataṉ poruḷ teḷivāki viṭṭāl ūḻiyattiṉ poruḷ teḷivāki viṭum.
aṭu eṉpataṉ vaḻi varum col aṭṭu, aṭutal eṉpatu cuṭutal, vāṭṭutal, vaṟuttal, porittal, camittal, kollutal, pōriṭal, tuṉpuṟuttal mutaliya poruḷkaḷit tarum. iṅkē aṭṭūḻiyam maṉattic cuṭutal, vāṭṭutal, tuṉpuṟuttal ākiya poruḷkaḷil iṭam peṟukiṟatu. maṉavetuppu, maṉavuḷivu ceyvatē aṭṭūḻiyamām; atuvum atikārattōṭu - atikāram illāta atikārattōṭu - irukkum eṉpatiyum niṉittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
nām ēviyatit kēṭṭu naṭakka vēṇṭiyavaṉ, nammi vēṇṭā vakiyil ēviyum maṉavuḷiccal tantum taṉtōṟṟatti mikuttuk koḷḷum pōtē uṉ aṭṭūḻiyatti niṟuttu eṉṟō , uṉ aṭṭūḻiyam aḷavukku viñciyatueṉṟō collum vaḻakku nērkiṉṟatu.
ūḻiyam ceypavarkaḷ taṅkaḷ ūḻiyatti oḻuṅkākac ceyyāmal avvūḻiyattiyum aḻittu, piṟar ūḻiyattiyum keṭuttu, ūḻiyatti oḻuṅkuṟuttum aluvalarkkum vēṇṭāk kuṟukkīṭum taṭiyumāy niṟkum etiriṭic ceyalkaḷic ceytal aṭṭūḻiyam eṉka.
aṭum paki niliyilum illi; ūḻiyam pārkkum niliyilum illi; iraṇṭum allāmal iraṇṭum iṇinta etiriṭi nili.
uḷḷattiṟku oṭṭiya niliyum illi; uḷḷattiṟku veṭṭiya niliyum illi; iraṇṭum allāmal iraṇṭum iṇinta etiriṭi nili!
aḻippavaṉāka iruntāl eccarikkiyāka iruntiruk kalām; aṇippavaṉāka vantu aḻippavaṉāka uḷḷam koṇṭiruppavaṉāl alikkaḻivu illāmal tīrumā?
vāḷpōl pakivar; kēḷ pōl pakivar eṉa iruvēṟu vakiyar pakivar. ivarō kēḷvāḷ pakivar! (tiruk.882).
kāvalukku ēṟpaṭuttap paṭṭavarē kaḷavāḷiyāka iruntu curaṇṭiṉāl eñcumā? payiri āṭu māṭu mēyāmal kākka amintatu vēli! atu payiri mēyntāl payir irukkumā? aḻukiyi amartta, amarttap paṭṭavaḷē uṟaṅkum piḷḷiyik kiḷḷi viṭupavaḷāka iruntāl collaṟiyāk kuḻanti eṉṉām? avaḷik kaṇṭālē aḻumē! avaḷip pārttālē ovvāmiyāki viṭumē!
aṭṭūḻiyam = aḻivu vēli; ūḻiyam ceytu kākka vēṇṭiyavarē, aḻikkum vēli; avvēliyi vittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭā eṉpatu pōlac collamiti amintuḷḷami aṟintu koḷḷat takkatu.
aṭṭi 
aṭu > aṭṭu + ai = aṭṭi.
aṭṭu 	= 	oṭṭik koḷvatu, oṭṭik kiṭappatu.
e-ṭu: 
aṭṭukkuṭṭi = tāyi viṭṭu akalāmal oṭṭikkiṭakkum āṭṭukkuṭṭi	aṭṭuppiṭittavaṉ = nīrāṭāmal aḻukkup piṭittavaṉ.
aṭṭukkuṭṭi pōl oṭṭikkiṭakkum ūruyiri ; nīrppata iṭattil kiṭantu kāl vippār aṟiyāmal kālil oṭṭik koṇṭu kurutiyi muṭṭa muṭṭa uṟiñci oṭṭiyatu teriyāmalē utirntu koḷvatu aṭṭiyiṉ iyalpām.
aṭṭipōl cuvippareṉpatu tivviya pirapanta akarāti.
puḻuti aṭṭic cemmiya kuṭam	-kampa. kiṭ.735
aṭṭiyāka oṭṭik koḷvāṉeṉṉum makkaḷ vaḻakku oṭṭik kiṭattalōṭu uṟiñcuvatiyum cuṭṭuvatām.
aṭṭikkuḻi 
taṇṭaṉi vakiyuḷ oṉṟu aṭṭikkuḻiyil taḷḷiviṭal.
aṇṇāttal ceyyātu aḷaṟu eṉpatu vaḷḷuvam. avvāṟu aṭṭikku uriya iṭamām. cēṟṟi viṭṭu veḷiyēṟāmiyoṭu aṭṭikkaṭiyāl uḷḷa kurutiyum uṟiñcappaṭṭu virintu muṭivi ēṟpaṭuttum.
aḷaṟu eṉpataṟku nirayam (narakam ) eṉṟa urikaḷi niṉika.
aṭṭiyāṭal 
aṭṭi tuṇṭikkappaṭṭālum tuṇṭāya uṭal pakuti tuḷḷit tuḷḷit tuṭikkum. avvāṟē pōrkkaḷattil taliyaṟṟa muṇṭam tuḷḷuvati aṭṭiyāṭal eṉṟaṉar.
aṭṭi aṟṟuḻiyum ūrumāṟu pōla uyirnīṅka aṟṟuḻiyum uṭampu āṭutaliṉ aṭṭiyāṭal eṉpa(tol. poruḷ. 71 nac.)
aṇaṅku 
aṇ > aṇa  > aṇaṅku.
tāṉē aṭuttu vantu nerukkit tuyarūṭṭuvatu.
aṇaṅkukol āymayil kollō	- tiruk. 1081
aruntiṟal aṇaṅkiṉ āviyar perumakaṉ	- ciṟupāṇ. 86
aṇaṅkē vilaṅkē kaḷvartam iṟiyeṉap
piṇaṅkal cālā accam nāṉkē	- tol. 1202
kaṇṇāl varuttum peṇ, varuttum teyvam, accuṟuttum pēy eṉpavi aṇaṅku eṉappaṭṭaṉa. tāyi makiḻac ceyvatuṭaṉ varuttavum ceyyum kuṭṭiyum aṇaṅku eṉappaṭum.
āḷiyiṉ aṇaṅku	- cilap. 25:  48
vayappaṭutti varuttuvatu. talivaṉum taliviyumāyk kūṭi irunta kāli iṉpuṟuttiya iyaṟki nalaṅkaḷ eviyō avi ellām avarkaḷ pirivuṟṟa kāli peruvaruttam ceyvati akattiṇip pālip pāṭalkaḷ vaḻiyē aṟika.
aṇattāṉ 
aṇattāṉ 	= 	ōṇāṉ. aṇ = mēlē.
taliyi mēlē tūkkit tōṟṟam tarutalāl aṇattāṉ eṉappaṭṭatu.
kataviṉ mēlē pōṭum tāḻ aṇṇāntāḻ eṉappaṭum. viḷiyāṭṭil tōṟṟavaṉukkut taṇṭaṉi vakiyāl ceyvatu aṇṇāntāḷ eṉpatu atu. kuṉiya vittu mutukiṉ mēl kallēṟṟutalām; viḷiyāṭṭu vaḻakkam itu.
aṇal 
aṇ = mēl, aṇṇam = mēlvāy, mēlvāyppakutiyil iruntu kaṉṉattil vaḻiyum mayirkkaṟṟi aṇal eṉpatu. tāṭi eṉpatum atu.
miyaṇaṟ kāḷi	- puṟam. 83
itaṉ poruḷ  mipōṉṟa tāṭiyiṉiyuṭi iḷiyōṉ (paḻiya uri).
aṇi 
aṇi: 1
oḻuṅkupaṭṭa amippuṭiyatu. aṇi = varici; aṇivakuppu, aṇitēr eṉpavaṟṟik karutuka. irupālum muḷiyaṭittu muḷikaḷil neṭuṅkayiṟṟik kaṭṭi variciyāka atil māṭukaḷik kaṭṭi niṟuttum vaḻakkattāl māṭṭuc cantikku tāmpaṇi eṉṟu peyar. tāmpu = kayiṟu; ciṟu tāmpu toṭutta pacalik kaṉṟueṉṉum mullippāṭṭil (12) tāmpu kayiṟātal aṟika. tāmpaṇi, tāmaṇiyāy, tāmaṇi tāvaṇiyāyc citivuṟṟu vaḻaṅkukiṟatu.
aṇi: 2
 oṉṟōṭu oṉṟu porunti aḻaku ceyvatu aṇi eṉappaṭum. paṭivīrar aṇivakuppi eṇṇuka! uyaram, uṭi, karuvi, naṭi eṉpavi oppap poruntum orumi uvakik kiṭamām. avvāṟē pāṭaṟkamiyum uvami mutalām aṇikaḷum, uṭaluṟuppukaḷoṭu porunta amiyum aṇikalaṅkaḷum poruntutalāl aṇi eṉappaṭuvaṉavām.
māṭakkuc cittiramum mānakarkkuk kōpuramum
āṭamitōḷ nallārk kaṇiyumpōl - nāṭimuṉ
aituriyā niṉṟa aṇinturiyi ennūṟkum
peyturiyāy vittār peritu
eṉṟu naṉṉūl (55) aṇinturi paṟṟi naviṉṟatu.
aṇiyam 
tayār eṉṉum ayaṟ collukku aṇiyam eṉṉum collip paṭittār pāvāṇar. aṇivakuppil niṟpār āṇikkuk kāttiruntu kaṭaṉāṟṟum nilipōl nōkki yiruppatu aṇiyam ākum.
iṉi, aṇmi iṭam aṇimi eṉappaṭuvatuṭaṉ aṇiyam eṉavum vaḻaṅkutal nelli vaḻakku. aṇiyam = pakkam.
aṇi yintakam ( alaṅkāra pañcakam) 
veṇpāvum, kalittuṟiyum, āciriyappāvum, āciriya viruttamum, vaṇṇamum eṉṉum aintum māṟi māṟi antātiyāka varappāṭutal aṇiyintakamām. alaṅkāra pañcakam (va). (pañcakam - aintuṭiyatu)
veṇpā akaval kalittuṟi akaval
viruttam canta viruttamiv vakiyē
māṟi māṟiyan tātit toṭiyāyp
pāṭuva talaṅkāra pañcaka mākum	- muttuvī. 1108
aṇil 		
aṇiyāvatu varici. aṇi + il = aṇil.
variciyāy mūṉṟu varikaḷiyuṭiya uyiri, ātalāl aṇil eṉappaṭṭatu.
mūvari aṇil
eṉṟār tolkāppiyar (tol. 1505).
veḷvarikkāyi vāḷvarik koṭuṅkāy eṉṟār iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ (16:25). koṭumi = vaḷivu. vaḷinta variyuṭiya veḷḷarikkāyum  vālvarik koṭuṅkāy eṉṟu pāṭamōti veḷvarikkāy eṉpārumuḷar. aṇil varik koṭuṅkāy eṉap puṟattiṉum (246) kāṭṭiṉāraṭiyārk.
aṇi vaki 
aṇi	= 	oṉṟi oṉṟu porunta aḻakuṭaṉ 			ceyyappaṭṭatu.

aṇu 
aṇ + u = aṇu.  ceṟivuṭiyatu aṇu.
piḷattaṟku ariyatām nuṇ ceṟivaminta aṭimūlam aṇuvām. oṉṟaṉōṭu oṉṟu aṇṇic ceṟinta nuṇmam aṇuveṉka.
aṇṇutal 	= 	neruṅkutal.
maṇ tiṇinta nilam eṉṉum puṟappāṭal uriyil (2) aṇucceṟinta nilameṉpār paḻiya uriyāciriyar.
aṇuttiraḷ oli
eṉpār naṉṉūlār. aṇuvip piḷattal paṟṟiyatu tiruvaḷḷuva mālip pāṭṭu oṉṟu. aṇuvit tuḷittu eṉpatu.
aṇuvic cata (nūṟu) kūṟiṭṭa kōṇ
eṉpār kampar (uyut. 254)
aṇi 
aṇ+ ai = aṇi = ceṟivu, nerukkam.
aṭuttut taḻuvutal, kaṭṭip piṭittal aṇitalām.
maṟṟik kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷilum valuvāy - ceṟivāy - kaṭṭappaṭṭa tiṇṇiya cuvar aṇikkaṭṭu eṉappaṭal aṟika. ēṟu taḻuvutal aṇital eṉappaṭum. uyar irukki, metti, talitāṅki āyavi aṇi eṉappaṭal uṇṭu. avi ariyaṇi, pañcaṇi, taliyaṇi eṉpaṉa.
ceṟivup paṇpum uyartal paṇpum uṭiyatu aṇi.
uyarvu akalam tiṇmi arumiin nāṉkum
amivaraṇ eṉṟurikkum nūl	- tiruk. 743
eṉpatu, aḵtu aṇikkum poruntuvatām. tuḷḷivarum veḷḷattit taṭuttu niṟutta vēṇṭum. valimi aṇikku vēṇṭum allavō!
aṇṭat tōṟṟaravu 
aṇṭam  + tōṟṟaravu = aṇṭat tōṟṟaravu = ulakat tōṟṟam.
nilam nīr tī vaḷi viṇ eṉṉum aimperum pūtaṅkaḷum kalanta mayakkam ulakam eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (1589). ataṉi virittuk kūṟukiṉṟatu paripāṭal.
karuvaḷar vāṉat ticiyil tōṉṟi
uruvaṟi vārā oṉṟaṉ ūḻiyum
untuvaḷi kiḷarnta ūḻūḻ ūḻiyum
centīc cuṭariya ūḻiyum paṉiyoṭu
taṇpeyal taliiya ūḻiyum aviyiṟ
ṟuṇmuṟi veḷḷa mūḻki yārtarupu
mīṇṭum pīṭuyarvu īṇṭi avaṟṟiṟkum
uḷḷī ṭākiya irunilat tūḻiyum
ulaki, iṟimiyākak kāṇum meyp poruḷāḷar,
pāriṭi aintāyp parantāy pōṟṟi
nīriṭi nāṉkāy nikaḻntāy pōṟṟi
tīyiṭi mūṉṟāyt tikaḻntāy pōṟṟi
vaḷiyiṭi iraṇṭāy makiḻntāy pōṟṟi
veḷiyiṭi oṉṟāy viḷintāy pōṟṟi
eṉṟu pāṭiṉār (tiruvācakam, pōṟṟit tiruvakaval) ivvāṟu. ulakam tōṉṟiya vakiyi virittuk kūṟum nūl aṇṭat tōṟṟaravu eṉpatām. aṇṭanili cāṟṟal aṇṭakōcam, vakuppuk kaliveṇpāvāmeṉpatu pirapantat tiraṭṭu.
aṇṭam 
aṇ = nerukkam. aṇ > aṇṭu  > aṇṭam.
neruṅkiya kōḷkaḷ palavaṟṟik koṇṭa ulakam kōḷkaḷ nerukkamā eṉiṉ, nāḷ mīṉām viṇmīṉ oṉṟaṟ koṉṟu koṇṭuḷḷa iṭiveḷiyi nōkka nerukkam eṉpatu pulappaṭum.
aṇṭam viti, muṭṭi eṉṉum poruḷ. nīḷ uruṇṭi vaṭiviṉa. avvaṭiyi uṭiya ulakap peyarāyiṟṟu. aṇṭam eṉpatu.
aṇṭap pakutiyiṉ uṇṭip piṟakkam	- tiruvā. tiruvaṇ. 1
aṇṭar 
aṇṭu + ar = aṇṭar.
aṇṭar eṉpār āyar; kuṟiñci nilatti aṇṭiyavar aṇṭar āyiṉar. avar ākkaḷ, erumikaḷ, āṭukaḷ āyavaṟṟukku aṇṭum iṭam tantaṉar. avi kāṭṭu vāḻviṉa eṉpatum, vīṭṭu vāḻviṉavākavum ākkappaṭṭavi eṉpatum, māntar tam nalam karutiyē. vīṭṭu vilaṅku ākkiṉar eṉiṉum kāṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷiṉ accam alippu āyavaṟṟukku iṭamillāmal toḻuvum paṭṭiyum toṭṭiyum amittu uṇṇavum parukavum vāyppukaḷ tantu pātukāttamiyāl avar aṇṭar āyiṉar.
kāṭṭup puliyiṉ koṭumi añci - num
	kālaṭi taṅkiya āṭukaḷi	
nāṭṭup puliyeṉak kolvatuvō - uṅkaḷ
	nāṉmaṟi ōtiya nītiaiyā!	
eṉpatu kavimaṇiyiṉ āciya cōti
aṇṭar makaḷir, aṇṭar makaṉ kuṟu vaḻutiyār eṉpaṉa caṅkanūl āṭciyum, caṅkap pulavar peyarumām. (puṟam. 346)
aṇṭal 
aṇ = nerukkam. aṇ  > aṇṭu  > aṇṭal.
aciyum poruḷ aciviṉṟi irukka aṇṭal (muṭṭu) koṭuppatu vaḻakkam. vaṇṭikaḷi niṟuttiṉāl cakkarattiṉ muṉṉum piṉṉum nerukkik kal, kaṭṭi āyavaṟṟi vippati aṇṭal koṭuttal eṉpar.
oruvariṭam oru poruḷik kaṭaṉāka vāṅkum pōtu ataṟku īṭāka ētāvatu uriyatu uḷḷatu eḻuttu āḷ piṇi eṉa vippati aṇṭal koṭuttal eṉpatum vaḻakkamām. aṇṭal paṇṇutal eṉpatum itu.
aṇṭā 
aṇṭutal = neruṅkutal; aṇṭāmi = neruṅkāmi. aṭuttu neruṅkāmal akaṉṟu virintu irukkum māḻikkalam aṇṭā eṉappaṭum. oruvar neruṅki iruntu uṟavit tuṇṭittuk koṇṭāl avaṉ aṇṭavē māṭṭāṉ eṉpar. aṇṭār = pakivar eṉappaṭalum āyiṉar.
aṇṭā eṉpatu pōṉṟatum ataṉiṉum ciṟitāy uruṇṭu tiraṇṭatāy iruppatumām kalam kuṇṭā eṉappaṭum. kuṇṭu = uruṇṭi.
aṇṭā kuṇṭā
eṉpatu iṇimoḻi.
aṇṭākuṇṭā 
aṇṭā 	= 	mikutiyāṉa aḷavil cōṟākkutalukkup payaṉpaṭuttum veṇkala ēṉam; koppari eṉpatum atu.
kuṇṭā 	= 	aṇṭāvil ākkap peṟṟa cōṟṟi aḷḷip pōṭṭup pantiyil parimāṟu taṟkup payaṉpaṭuttum ēṉam.
kalavakiyic cērntavi ivi, vaṭiva amippāl iraṇṭum ottavi. parumi ciṟumiyāl vēṟupaṭṭavi. aṇṭāviṟkuk kippiṭi vaḷiyaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. kuṇṭāviṟku avi illi. uṭalnala muṟippaṭi
aṇṭāc cōṟu aṇṭak kūṭātu
eṉpar. peruñcamiyal, vīṭṭuc camiyal pōṉṟa aḷaviṉatum amiviṉatum alla ākalāl avvāṟu kūṟiṉar.
aṇṭi 
aṇṭi	 = 	neruṅki. oruvari aṭuttu allatu neruṅki vāḻvatu aṇṭi vāḻtalām.
aṇṭip piḻikkum nammi āṭu
eṉpār pāratiyār (pāppāp pāṭṭu)
aṇṭi vīṭu, ayal vīṭueṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
kuṭali aṇṭit tōṉṟi veḷippaṭṭu varuttum kaṭṭiyi aṇṭi eṉpar. aṇṭi taḷḷal eṉpatu ataṉ peyar muṉtiri (muntiri)yil veḷippaṭak kīḻē turutti niṟkum vitip pakutiyiyum aṇṭiyiyum nōkki ataṉi aṇṭi māṅkoṭṭi eṉpar.
aṇṭiraṉ 
aṇ + tiraṉ = aṇṭiraṉ.
aṇṇutal = ceṟital; aṇi = mēl.
ceṟintaminta āṟṟuluṭiyāṉ aṇṭiraṉ eṉap paṭṭāṉ. āy aṇṭiraṉ eṉpāṉ vaḷḷal eḻuvaruḷ oruvaṉ (puṟam. 129  136)
mōcikīraṉārāl pāṭappaṭṭavaṉ ātalāl.
mōci pāṭiya āyum	- puṟam. 158
eṉac caṅkac cāṉṟōrāl pukaḻappaṭṭāṉ.
maḻitavaḻ potiyil avaṉ āṭcip pakuti āykkuṭi āṅkuḷa ūr, kaṇtiraḷ nōṉkāḻ, kaṇṭiraḷ nōṉkāḻ ākum. avvāṟē, aṇ tiraṉ, aṇṭiraṉām. (puṟam. 95)
aṇṭi 
aṭuttiruppavarum aṭuttirukkum vīṭum aṇṭi eṉṟum aṇṭi vīṭu eṉṟum aṇṭi vīṭṭukkārar eṉṟum vaḻaṅkap peṟuvar.
aṇṭi ayal
eṉpatu iṇiccol. aṇṭiyār = nerukkamikka uṟaviṉarām.
aṇṭi ayal 
aṇṭi 	= 	taṉ vīṭṭi aṇṭi (neruṅki) irukkum vīṭṭār.
ayal 	= aṇṭi vīṭṭukku aṭuttirukkum vīṭṭār.
aṇ = nerukkam; aṇukkam = nerukkam; aṇukkar = naṇpar; aṇimi, aṇmi ivaṟṟāl aṇṭi nerukkap poruḷātal koḷka. ayalār = piṟar; tiruvaruṭku ayalumāy eṉpār tāyumāṉavar. ayal, appāl eṉṉum poruṭṭatām.
koṇṭavarkaḷ koṭuttavarkaḷ, aṇṭiyavarkaḷ aṭuttavarkaḷ ellā makkaḷum nallā irukkaṇum eṉpatu makkaḷ vāḻttu vakiyuḷ oṉṟu.
aṇṇaṇittu 
kūṭakkuṟiya eṉṟum ēṟattāḻa eṉṟum nām vaḻaṅku kiṉṟa coṟkaḷukkuriya poruḷit tarum collāka īḻattamiḻar aṇṇaṇittu eṉṉum colli vaḻaṅku kiṉṟaṉar.
aṇ = mēl;aṇittu = pakkam, nerukkam.
mēlniliyi oṭṭiyatāy uḷḷa nili aṇṇaṇittu eṉa vaḻaṅkutal, ilakkiya nayam, tūymi, poruṭceṟivu uṭiyatāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
aṇṇam 
aṇ + am = aṇṇam.
aṇ = mēl; aṇṇam = mēlvāy
nuṉinā aṇari aṇṇam varuṭa
